## Wave 4 landing notes (Agent 2)
- Repository shape: module functions taking the handle first, plus `*_in(conn, …, now=…)` forms for use inside a bigger transaction (lead decision for wave 4, replaces "methods on the handle").
- Merge fixes: `sessions.create_in` also accepts `created_source` and the lineage flags (`archived`, `auto_archived`, `hidden`, `pinned`) that continuations and branches carry; an untitled parent's first branch is titled `branch` (a free base is returned as is), `#2` from the second on.
- `TYPED_BUCKETS` rows added: compression_parent_ended → compression_closed, session_guarded → compression, session_not_found → session_row_missing, vacuum_refused → locked.
- Accepted builder decisions: handoff rows marked `finish_reason = 'indus.handoff'`; appends refuse a compression-ended session (`store.compression_closed`), the lock is never checked; branch `model_config` = only `_branched_from`; tombstoned id in an import refuses the whole import; store without FTS5 searches with LIKE; doctor label keys `word_yes`/`word_no`.
- Open: `sessions.export_message_limit` is read but not in the config schema; FTS stale rebuild at open can wait up to 120 s on the cross-process lock; CI needs a `slow` lane for `tests/integration/state` (the stress test runs 60 s per mode by default; `INDUS_STRESS_SECONDS` shortens it).
- P02-21: D-04.6 verdict "stdlib sqlite3 holds" (DELETE ~310 appends/s, WAL-pinned ~630; numbers in `docs/notes/P02/stress-test.md`).
## Wave 5 landing notes (Agent 2) — P04-01..12, 17
- `ExitReason` lives in `indus.protocol.events` (protocol is below core); P04-15's `core/exit_reasons.py` re-exports it. `protocol/__init__.py` and `tools/__init__.py` stay one-line docstrings (layers test).
- Wire shapes: events are notifications whose method is the event type; server requests go out as a `server_request` notification and are answered with `server_request.answer`; own error codes use bands 1xxx–6xxx by group; `error_for_indus_code()` maps product codes (for P04-21).
- Biome now ignores `tests/fixtures/captures` (recorder output); this fixes the red `pnpm lint` CI step since P03.
- Prompt: all prompt prose in `core/prompt/texts.py` (steer markers `STEER_OPEN`/`STEER_CLOSE` there; P04-19 imports them); config under a new `prompt.*` section; `sessions.set_system_prompt(_in)` added for rebuilt prompts; the install-tree rule means running inside the Indus checkout loads no project context.
- Response checks: nudge texts are in `response_checks.NUDGES` (could move to `prompt/texts.py`); verdicts (`drop_scaffold`, `append`, `output_cap`, `wait`, `reasoning_off`) are applied by P04-15; the repair report's `rewritten`/`flushed` by P04-16. `ProviderProfile` has no wire-rule/think-tag fields yet (P08).
- Tools: registration conflicts raise (`ToolNameTaken`/`ToolSchemaInvalid`); built-in modules expose `register_tools(registry)` via `BUILTIN_MODULES` (empty until P05); `dispatch.run_sync` is the interim D-04.1 blocking helper (the prompt slice has a private one too: unify in P04-23); the tool round builds its spill sink with `spill.host_spill_for(scope, lambda: spill.store_references(store))`.
## Wave 6a landing notes (Agent 2) — P04-22, P04-23 (RouteBinding), P04-21 (lock + broker), D-04.1 blocking helper
- Server-request broker interface is `indus.protocol.requests_port.ServerRequestPort` (core/tools may not import service); `service.broker.ServerRequestBroker` implements it. Default answerable kinds are empty: a client declares what it can answer (R05.3); the in-process REPL host declares all four. `session.busy` has no locale copy yet (surface adds it).
- Session lock files live in `<store dir>/session-locks/`, non-blocking (`try_lock_session` → `SessionLock | LockBusy`).
- Goldens: every `tools.json` is `[]` until P05 registers tools; regenerate with `uv run pytest tests/golden/prompt_surface --update-golden` and record why.
- `RouteBinding` rewrites only the prompt's identity lines on a switch; reasoning/extra body/cache switch read from free-form `providers.<name>` config; cache markers only on `anthropic_messages` until P08.
- `foundation/blocking.py` is the one D-04.1 blocking helper (pools io 8 / tools 8 / hooks 2, daemon threads, deadline abandonment, context copied). A full pool refuses (`PoolFull`) — check under D-04.1 criterion (c); if it bites, let `io`/`tools` wait for a slot up to the deadline.
- 6b: D-04.1 criteria suite needs a `perf` marker in pyproject; the Agent reads `binding.state` once per request and drops a retry whose generation changed (R01.5).
- Core loop (slice X, P04-13..16, 18, 19): the Agent keeps one `SessionRuntime` per session and per turn calls `new_turn_state(runtime, message=…, turn_id=…, generation=…, persist_text=…, history=None, sink=…, broker=…, next_seq=…, budget=…)` then `await run_turn(state)`; interrupt/steer/redirect go through `state.control` on the loop thread. Store writes go through `core/persist.py` (P02 adapter + in-memory store); provider calls through `core/model_call.py` (`RouteCaller` = P03 `retry_call(stream_call)`; `agent.api_max_retries` not wired yet — the Agent passes it).
- Open from slice X: `ExitReason` has no `internal_error` (loop bugs end `provider_failed` with reason `loop_error`/`local_error`) — consider adding it; steer rows are recognised on reload by marker text (no `display_kind` column — consider one); `LoopOptions` limits have no config keys yet; loop wording in `core/loop_texts.py`. Added behaviour: on resume, an open tool-call tail gets stored "outcome unknown" results so a side effect is never silently repeated.
## Wave 6b landing notes (Agent 2) — P04-20, P04-21 rest, D-04.1 (e)
- Agent construction is async (`Agent.start`); `run_turn`/`chat` are coroutines; synchronous callers (classic REPL, P06) go through `InProcessHost`, which declares all four answerable kinds and runs calls under the host's scope.
- Session lock is held per attachment, not per turn; `create` writes no row (the first turn's prologue does). `RouteCaller.still_current` drops a retry once the route generation moves (R01.5).
- Open: `session.create` `title` accepted but not stored (wire it to `titles.set_title` in P06/P07); `ToolContext` has no progress hook yet (`EventSink.tool_progress` ready for P05); task cleanups run only at `close()` — P05 backends decide whether they need a turn-end hook.
- **D-04.1 decided (lead, Agent 2): asyncio core kept.** All five criteria pass against the real loop (a 0.13–0.65 ms stream close; b abandoned at 1.008 s, heartbeat gap ≤ 19 ms; c 50 sessions p99 5.6 ms; d hung hook isolated; e sync caller via InProcessHost). Numbers: `docs/notes/P04/d-04-1-decision.md`. Fixes taken: `io`/`tools` pools queue up to 256 waiters within their deadline (`hooks` still refuses), slot released before the result is returned. Proposals: P13-17 give `hooks` a bounded wait queue or 8 workers; move `StateStore.load` to the `io` pool; P05 adds the terminal SIGTERM→SIGKILL(+2 s) backstop with its own criterion-(b) test against the real `terminal` tool.
## P05 wave 1 landing notes (Agent 2) — P05-01..07, 22..24
- Backends API (for P05-11/12 terminal tool and P05-15 file ops): `indus.backends.contract` (`Environment.execute(command, *, timeout, cwd, stdin, bounded=True, on_activity, cancel)`, `ExecResult`, `BackendFlags`, errors) and `indus.backends.registry` (`acquire`, `run`, `release`, `shutdown`). **File ops must pass `bounded=False`.** `cancel` is any object with `is_set()` (fits `TurnControl.stop`). The host (P06/service) must call `EnvironmentRegistry.shutdown()` at exit. P05-10 hooks sudo into `SpawnedEnvironment._prepare`; P05-13 keeps job files out of `<home>/cache/terminal`.
- D-04.1 criterion (b) against a real child: `tests/integration/core/test_d04_1_terminal_backstop.py` (ordered kill 1.5 s, backstop force-kills by timeout + 2 s).
- Safety API: `safety.child_env.child_env(scope, *, variant="default"|"minimal"|"code", extra=None, session_id=None)` (the only source of child environments; `no_raw_environ` guard with an allow-list file); `safety.redaction.redact_output(text, *, command=None, force=False, scope=None)`; `safety.approvals.detect(command, …) -> Detection` over our own 594-row corpus. Short mask stays `[redacted]` (P01). Call `register_blocked_names("providers", bundled.env_var_names())` at service start. New config: `terminal.env_passthrough` (lead, merge).
- Merge fixes: backends now use `platform.locate.which` and `known_dirs.homebrew_bins` (lookup ratchet R09.18); the backend's stand-in `build_child_env(variant="terminal")` became `child_env()`.
- Web: optional extra `web = ["ddgs==9.16.0"]` (MIT); the licence check walks only `[project].dependencies`, so extras are checked by hand — extend the check (follow-up). Goldens regenerated with compact JSON (`protocol.codegen.dump_json`); `web_search` is hidden in goldens because its probe needs the extra.
- P05 left for Agent 1: P05-08 approval pipeline, 09 human approval, 10 sudo, 11/12 terminal tool, 13/14 background processes, 15-19 file tools (not started), 20 V4A + lint, 21 search_files. Then P06 (Demo 1).
## P05 wave 2 landing notes (Agent 1) — P05-08..21
- Lead decision D-04.7: import layers put tools above backends (backends never import tools).
- V4A runs through `patch` (any model may send it); the schema still shows only the replace shape — route-aware schema is a P06 seam. PatchFiles is async; `read_text` → None when missing; `refusal(path, action, content)`.
- Lint: `write_file` and `patch` run `lint.gate` before and `lint.lint_edit` after; result `lint` field only when introduced/preexisting.
- Approvals: `ApprovalDecision.clear_stale_interrupt(flag)` is a method (not a module function). Unattended runs never prompt for sudo. A call with no broker counts as unattended → `approvals.unattended_mode` (default deny).
- Tool round extras: `cancel`, `broker`, and `environments` when `SessionRuntime.environments` is set (host sets it).
- P06 host-wiring seams: (1) `SessionRuntime.environments = terminal.runtime().environments` so terminal/file tools/search share one registry and cwd; (2) offer `extras["run_context"]` (cron/one-shot/unattended); (3) `await terminal.runtime().shutdown()` at exit; (4) per-session completion sink in Deliveries checking `ProcessRegistry.consumed(...)`; (5) dispatcher calls `read_tracking.note_other_tool(scope, task_id)` after non-read tools; P08 calls `compaction_boundary`; (6) route-aware patch schema for V4A-preferring families.
- Open: THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES row for `ddgs` (web extra); unidentified one-off test failure on 2026-10-18 17:55 (4 clean reruns; full runs now use `-rf`).
## P06 landing notes (Agent 1) — classic CLI, Demo 1
- REPL entry: `indus.cli.launch:run_launch` builds the SlashContext and calls `indus.cli.repl.run_repl(..., slash_ctx=, on_resume=, on_ready=)`; slash and `!` lines run in a terminal section (prompt put away, real stdout, Ctrl+C reaches the child).
- `/model` writes `model_config["model_override"]`; resume reads it before the launch record.
- Security: `tool.started` arguments are redacted at the source (`safety.redaction.redact_arguments`); the renderer masks again.
- `-z`: stdout carries only a model reply; when the turn failed, the loop's failure notice goes to stderr with the `indus -z:` prefix (lead fix at landing).
- Ledger check now fails a cited test that is skipped for good (D-15.3).
- Open seams for P07/P08: status bar has no context/cost/jobs data; `/new` after resume has no launch record; in-REPL `/resume` restores model but not reasoning level; bang uses the session row cwd, not the terminal's live cwd; banner skill count is 0 until P09; `-z` usage-file token counters null until P08; bare `-c` hint names `indus sessions list` (arrives in P07).
## P07 landing notes (lead) — CLI commands, slices A–D + P07-26
- Order applied: B (auth), A (setup/guards/model/fallback), C (config/profile/tools/approvals/completion), D (sessions/doctor/logs/status/dump/backup/import/checkpoints). `REGISTRY` holds all 18 top-level commands in research 04 §3.2.4 order (chat, model, fallback, setup, auth, status, doctor, approvals, dump, backup, checkpoints, import, config, tools, sessions, profile, completion, logs); `lazy.py` always lists the registry one-liner (A and C made the same fix, kept once); D's `test_app.py` parsing workaround dropped.
- One prompt kit: `cli.prompts` gained `io_for(state, guidance)` (swappable `io_factory`, tests script it) and `confirm(io, q, assume_yes=)`; B's click prompts, C's tools checklist / profile delete, D's `cmdkit.confirm` and sessions browse now use it (`cmdkit` keeps only non-prompt helpers). Terminal = stdin and stdout both.
- Sign-ins: the picker offers "Sign in" for every key-mint flow of `providers.auth.flows` (`model_picker.sign_in_for`, lazy). `AuthStore.signed_in()` is the one reader for "signed in" (first-run guard, `status`, `dump`).
- Tools: setup's tools section goes through `tools.enable_names/disable_names` (`PlatformToolsModel`); chat reads `platform_toolsets.cli` via `runtime.toolset_selection(policy, scope)` when no `-t` (not in safe mode / ignore-user-config).
- Credentials: profile clones skip pool secrets (`pool_model.is_pool_secret`); `config unset NAME` prunes NAME's stored env-seeded pool rows (`prune_env_rows`).
- Doctor: config check = `config_check.check_settings` (findings carry a `topic`; a newer-than-build file is an error in both); `--fix` seeds via `config_read.seed_config`; `--minimal` "selected by" exact through `bootstrap.run_selected` → `CliState.selected_by`.
- Schema rows added: `platform_toolsets`, `security.acked_advisories`, `doctor.live_probe_timeout_s` (with A's `fallback_providers`, `onboarding.setup_completed`, `security.allow_data_training_tiers_noninteractive` and B's `credential_pool_strategies`).
- Fixes found at landing: `indus backup` defaulted to the process's home folder (`os.path.expanduser`), so an in-process run wrote into the real home — now the scope's `HOME`; the P01 writer appended list items after the trailing commented example block — the comment now moves to the new last item; C's `shutil.which` calls (config edit, profile alias) now go through `indus.platform.locate.which` (lookup ratchet); `tests/integration/cli` and `tests/contract/cli` reuse the unit CLI conftest (fresh terminal runtime / open foreground fence) — A's first-run integration test left the fence up and broke later terminal backstop tests in a full run.
- Intended differences recorded: D-16.93 (import refuses the whole archive) … D-16.100 (logs follow reopens), §2.7.
- P07-26: `tests/contract/cli/` — table of every command's no-argument non-TTY exit code (a new command without a row fails), 5 s watchdog, stdin tripwire, help golden order, `--help` per command, lazy-import isolation per top-level command.
- Open seams for P08+: (1) the runtime route still reads env-named keys; P08 selection must use `load_pool` / `peek` / `mark_exhausted` / `note_request`, and pool-only keys (`indus auth add`) do not make a provider usable for chat or the first-run guard until then; (2) `first_run.FLAGGED_ROUTES` is empty until profiles carry pricing / data-policy metadata (P08); (3) no bundled provider has a device-code flow yet; (4) checkpoint store protocol (`backends.checkpoint_api`) waits for P11's store (`set_store_factory`); (5) gateway / scheduled rows in `status` are placeholders (P14/P15); (6) `DEPRECATED_ENV` and the doctor check registry are the extension points later phases fill; (7) owner item: the timed three-minute `indus setup` run with a real key (M2).
- (lead, landing) Perf-budget tests are load-sensitive: `tests/trials/async_core/test_real_transports.py::test_fifty_sessions_through_the_driver_p99` (p99 < 50 ms) and `tests/integration/core/test_d04_1_criteria.py::test_b_*` failed 4/6 alone while 3 builders ran test suites on the same 4-core machine, then passed 6/6 each when load dropped; base passed throughout. Follow-up: give them a `perf` marker and a serial CI lane (no xdist, no parallel jobs on the runner).
- (lead) A test run wrote `/root/indus-backup-2026-01-01-090000.zip` (506 bytes, a temp profile's PERSONA.md + profile.yaml) before the backup default-folder bug was fixed; left for the owner to delete.

## P08 wave 1 landing notes (lead) — P08-01..11, 16, 17, 19 (slices A, B, C)
- Order applied on main (P07 landed): B (usage/cost, liveness, aux client, `@` refs), C (cache plan, Responses, Bedrock, Gemini), A (anchor, threshold, boundaries, summary, engine, overflow). Conflicts only in `core/agent.py` imports, `core/turn_state.py` (`run_budget` + `context`) and `config/schema.py` (union: `auxiliary`, `prompt_caching`, `compression`, `context`; `test_schema` now pins all four default trees). No dependency change, no re-lock.
- One usage path: `RecordingCaller` → `UsageLedger` → listeners. Listeners now hear every answered call (usage may be None) and are awaited; the engine registers `on_usage`, the loop only announces the priced rows (`expect_usage`); A's direct `observe_usage` is gone. Side calls carry a task tag and never touch the anchor; the main-route summary (no aux client) is recorded as `compression`.
- Aux client: `cli/runtime.build_service` makes one `AuxClient` per run → `SessionService(aux=)` → `AgentOptions.aux`; each agent binds it to its own route and ledger (`AuxClient.bound`). Auto-title uses the bound client. `AuxSummarizer` adapted to `AuxResult` and the async `window_for(task, main_window)` (asked once at session start; the `auto` route answers the main window without a lookup). A's test-only aux shape replaced by a test over the real client.
- `/compress [focus] [here N] [--preview]` wired: slash → `SessionPort.compress` (`CompressReport`) → `SessionService.compress` → `Agent.compress`.
- Rotating compaction adopted: `SessionRuntime.on_continued` → Agent moves sink, ledger, engine (anchor + counters) and remembers former ids for cleanups → `SessionService` re-keys, keeps the old id as an alias (`current_id`), moves the lock when free → REPL follows the first event with the new id, slash context follows the REPL; `-z` reports the final session id.
- `ResponseCancelled` (bare or inside `RetryFailed`) ends the turn `interrupted_by_system`, no failure. `@` reference budget = room under the compression trigger after the last reported prompt (window share only when compression is off). `-z --usage-file`, the REPL status bar (`launch.status_from_agent`, first time a status source is wired) and `/usage` (cost + status + cache hit share) read the ledger/store; an unpriced route is never shown as `$0.00`.
- Bundled `gemini` profile added (alias `google`, native wire, `GEMINI_API_KEY`/`GOOGLE_API_KEY`). Bedrock profile deferred: region URL + AWS chain do not fit the one-key bundled-vendor rule.
- `usage.normalize` now accepts our mode name `bedrock` (B wrote `bedrock_converse`).
- Flake watch: `test_search_files.py::test_paging_with_next_offset` failed 3/21 alone here and 1/12 on base main — ripgrep's parallel walker order differs per run, so offset paging across calls repeated/skipped hits (real P05 bug). Content search now uses `--sort=path`; 25/25 alone after. D-16.108.
- Intended differences recorded: D-16.101 (cache marker rule) … D-16.108 (search path order), §2.7.
- Perf budget (6× alone each, idle machine): `test_fifty_sessions_through_the_driver_p99` 6/6 here (0.53–0.74 s after a 5.7 s cold first run) vs 6/6 on main (0.55–0.71 s); `test_d04_1_criteria.py::test_b_*` (3 tests) 6/6 here (2.77–3.34 s) vs 6/6 on main (2.77–3.24 s). No slowdown from the ledger, cache plan or liveness on the hot path.
- Seams for P08 wave 2: (12) pool runtime — `BoundCaller.caller_for` builds one `RouteCaller` per request from `binding.state`; pool selection/rotation belongs before `RouteCaller` (P07 `load_pool`/`peek`/`mark_exhausted`/`note_request`), the ledger already records per route; (13) fallback chain — `RouteBinding.apply` + `_follow_route` keep runtime, tools, engine (`set_route` resets cooldown/strikes) and aux (`auto` follows the binding) in step; `AuxClient` already walks `fallback_providers`; R01.10 open; restore primary in the prologue; (14) smart approvals — `Agent.aux.call("approval", …)` (30 s default, `auxiliary.approval`), `tests/fakes/aux_model.py` to extend with verdicts; usage lands under `approval`; (15) fast mode — `RecordingCaller` already passes `service_tier` into the raw usage and pricing treats an unknown served tier as `unknown`; the request body change belongs in `BoundCaller.call` next to `finish_request`; register `/fast` in the P06 registry; (18) soak — `TurnKit` has no ledger; drive an `Agent` (as `tests/unit/core/test_agent_p08.py`) so cost/usage totals and compaction counts are the real path.
- Follow-ups (recorded in notes): response headers not passed by the stream driver (rate-limit buckets empty); `cap_timeout` not applied to P03 implicit timeouts; `--run-budget` flag not wired (config only); DeepSeek/xAI/Mistral/Bedrock unpriced; Bedrock `inputTokens` assumption to confirm with a live capture; all P08 transport captures synthetic (D-04.5); `session.compressed` is a `Notice` key, not a typed event; P03 classifier output-budget wording not mapped (core.overflow reads it).

## P11 landing notes (lead) — Docker, SSH, sync, processes, checkpoints, worktrees (slices A, B, C + P11-21)
- Order applied on main (P08 wave 1 landed): A (Docker 01–07), B (SSH, sync, credential files, remote processes, receipts, crash recovery 08–14), C (checkpoints, rollback, prune, worktrees 15–20). Conflicts A×B only: `backends/registry.py` (rows local, docker, ssh), `cli/doctor/checks.py` (docker and ssh branches), `config/schema.py` (docker/container and ssh/credential rows), contract conftest, `test_registry.py`, both ledgers (unions). C applied clean. No dependency change, no re-lock; `pyproject.toml` registers the `docker` marker.
- SECURITY (fixed at landing): the sudo hook is keyed by backend environment (`SudoSupport.target`, cache key `(home, session, target)`); a host-local hook never rewrites on a non-host-local environment; Docker and SSH get a hook only through `sudo.backend_sudo` when `docker_sudo_password` / `ssh_sudo_password` is stored — that hook reads only its own secret and never prompts. This machine's password never reaches a remote host or a container (D-16.134; tests in `test_sudo.py`, the ssh environment and docker recovery tests).
- Host wiring: `TerminalRuntime.create` registers Docker recovery's lost handler (weak ref to the process registry, unregistered at shutdown) → `mark_lost`; new `service/terminal_host.py` (`install(store, processes)`, called by `cli/runtime.build_service`) sets the receipts lineage resolver (`state.compression.lineage_ids`) and the orphan reaper's liveness (session over = newest chain segment ended; unknown/missing/error = alive, since the answers are process-wide). Gateway hosts (P15) call the same `install`.
- Remote polls only peek at the one-shot `environment_recreated` (put back with `mark_recreated`); jobs carry the environment's `recreations` at start and a replaced sandbox's jobs are lost and never signalled.
- SSH interrupt/timeout now also signals the remote process group (pid file in the remote temp dir, TERM → local ordered kill → KILL, 5 s bounds); with the link down the remote command may run on (recorded).
- Credential files: A's `importlib` stand-in called `mount_specs(resolve(scope))` with one argument, so it always raised and nothing was mounted; now a direct import with `mount_specs(files, "/root/.indus")`.
- Found by P11-21: the local backend froze passthrough values into its snapshot (R02.1) — `LocalEnvironment._excluded_names` now excludes them.
- Hygiene: the fake ssh/docker clients run on this machine; SSH tests (and the contract rows) point the remote/sandbox temp dir at the test's own directory instead of `/tmp`. Earlier runs left `indus-snap-*` / `indus_bg_proc_*` files in `/tmp` (not deleted: other processes' live snapshots share the pattern). No test wrote under `/root` (find -newer marker before/after the full run).
- P11-21: contract rows `docker` and `ssh` over the fake clients (12 shared cases × local/docker/ssh/fake) + `test_docker_ssh_contract.py` (R02.1 for local/docker/ssh, argv R02.7, recreated warning, background start/poll/kill, degraded on a lost link). Live lanes `tests/integration/backends/test_docker_live.py` (marker `docker`, runs where the daemon and `INDUS_LIVE_DOCKER_IMAGE` are present, never pulls) and `test_ssh_live.py` (markers `docker` + `network`, opt-in `INDUS_LIVE_SSH=1`, builds `tests/fixtures/backends/sshd`); both skip here. A CI lane that pre-pulls and sets the variables is still to add.
- Intended differences recorded: D-16.120 … D-16.135, §2.7 (D-16.109–119 left free for P08 wave 2).
- Recorded for later phases: `approvals.destructive_slash_confirm` has no schema row (P15 adds it); `/rollback`, `/checkpoints`, `/worktree` handlers for TUI/desktop/gateway → P12/P20/P15; `/worktree new` does not move the running session; GHCR owner of the default sandbox image still owner-open (D-16.64).
- Open seams: the docker availability probe runs `docker version` synchronously (cached 30 s); a profile-wide container mounts only the credential files resolved for the session that created it; the safety floor's "sudo password configured" test still reads the host secret names for every backend (blocks agent-written `sudo -S`, no leak).
## P08 wave 2 landing notes (lead) — P08-12, 13 (slice A), P08-14, 15, 18 (slice B)
- Order applied: A (credential pool runtime, fallback chain), then B (smart approvals, fast mode, soak), both from 822b6cd. Conflicts only in `core/agent.py` (imports, `BoundCaller` fields, `Agent.__init__`, `_open_client`, `BoundCaller.call`) and `core/turn_state.py` (`SessionRuntime.fallback` + `approval_judge`, both kept). `config/schema.py` / `test_schema` merged cleanly (`agent.fast_mode`, `agent.fast_window_s`, `approvals.denial_breaker_threshold`, `approvals.smart_policy`, new `fallback.min_switch_reset_s`); ledgers (acceptance map, traps) merged cleanly; `pyproject.toml` gains the `live` marker. No dependency change, no re-lock.
- `BoundCaller.call` order: `finish_request` (cache plan) → fast tier (`FastMode.layer` on the route state read once) → `PoolRuntime.call` (or the plain `RouteCaller` when the provider has no pool) → `fast_mode.served`. The pool swaps only `api_key` / `key_env`, so the tier's headers ride on every leased key; the chain's eager rule and the pool's per-attempt rule wrap P03's classifier in `caller_for`.
- Soak: B's stand-in `tests/integration/core/_stubs_b.py` deleted; the 429 storm runs through a real two-key pool (P07 rows + secrets) and a real `fallback_providers` entry (`custom` + `base_url`, written to the profile's `config.yaml`). Wire assertions unchanged; the stand-in's event list became the chain's two `fallback` notices. Fixture: storm `Retry-After` 1 → 10 s (a 1 s provider reset lapses while the driven fake clock moves during the bench write, so the first key came back before the second was leased); test profile carries `context_length_hint` (vendor profiles do not read `/models`).
- Fast mode verified against the vendors' docs (2026-10-18, `docs/notes/P08/fast-mode.md`): Anthropic `speed: "fast"` + `fast-mode-2026-02-01` beta (required for Opus 5 / 4.8, accepted on 5.5), models `claude-opus-5-5`, `claude-opus-5`, `claude-opus-4-8`; OpenAI `service_tier: "priority"` (Fast mode since 2026-07-30, `fast` also accepted, responses report `priority`), models = the 22 ids of the pricing page's Fast table. Matching changed from substring to exact id (the old substrings reached `gpt-5-nano`, `gpt-5.4-nano`, `o3-mini`).
- Decisions accepted: smart-approval judge as a plain async callable (safety may not import providers); `/fast` state per process; denial breaker per `ApprovalState`; `fallback.min_switch_reset_s` default 0; `PER_MODEL_RATE_LIMITS = {"anthropic"}`.
- Intended differences recorded: D-16.109 (sticky pool selection), D-16.110 (same-key retry inside P03's loop), D-16.111 (eager fallback only on `upstream_rate_limit`), D-16.112 (no short cap for a sole credential's cooldown), D-16.113 (fast tier from profile declarations only), D-16.114–115 (smart approvals), §2.7. (D-16.120–135 are P11's.)
- Follow-ups (in notes): `invalid_tool` strikes end the turn instead of walking the chain; add a `model_entitlement` reason to the P03 classifier (pool uses `ENTITLEMENT_PHRASES`); streamed responses carry no served `service_tier` / `usage.speed` (requested tier recorded with `fast_requested`); approval observer hooks around the judge (P13); two-hour live session (owner); a short provider reset lets the pool cycle keys until its per-call budget (7 tries with two keys) before the chain — by design, watch cost.
- Seams for P09/P10: (P10 delegation) pass the parent's `Agent.pools` as `AgentOptions.pools` so children share live credentials, never the parent's `FallbackChain` (each child builds its own from config); the child's aux client gives it its own approval judge. (P09 surfaces) `Notice(key="fallback")` texts ("Model fallback: …", "Main model restored …") are the durable fallback events to show; `/fast` lives in `cli/slash/control_cmds.py` and `fast_mode.FAST_MODES` keyed (profile home, session id) — gateway/desktop toggles use the same table; `approvals.mode: smart` needs `AgentOptions.aux` wired by the host, otherwise smart falls back to asking the person.

## Gate change (lead, 2026-10-19): perf tests run alone
- The D-04.1 wall-clock tests (`-m perf`, `test_d04_1_criteria.py`) failed in four full runs in a row
  while builders shared the machine, and passed every time run alone (marker text: numbers are only
  meaningful run alone). The gate is now `pytest -m "not perf"` for the full suite, then
  `pytest -m perf` on its own. CI does the same (separate step; the py3.14 job skips perf). The
  bounds themselves are unchanged.
## P10 slices G+H landing notes (lead) — P10-01..07 delegation, P10-08..11 execute_code and kernels
- Order applied on main d8ad78b (P09 waves 1–2 landed): H (`execute_code` runner/RPC, in-script module, local session kernels, remote kernels), then G (`delegate_task`, child builder, batch, output schema, background, teardown/hand-off, subagent worktrees), each `git apply --3way` from 85cd8a9. Conflicts, all unions: `tools/toolsets.py` (CORE_TOOLS = P05 set + P09 `memory`, `session_search`, `skills_list`, `skill_view`, `skill_manage` + `execute_code` + `delegate_task`; toolsets `memory`, `session_search`, `skills`, `code_execution`, `delegation`), `test_toolsets.py`, `tests/unit/cli/setup/test_wizard.py` (bundle rows now clarify, code_execution, delegation, file, memory, search, session_search, skills, terminal, todo, web), `config/schema.py` + test (`code_execution`, `delegation` after P09's sections), `core/agent.py` (`note_sources` + `only_tools`/`delegation_depth`; `before_turn`/`after_turn`/`note_sources` hooks all kept), `tools/builtin/__init__.py` (`code`, `delegate`), traps ledger (R02.32 rows). `tests/golden/prompt_surface/*/tools.json` regenerated with `uv run pytest tests/golden/prompt_surface --update-golden` (adds `delegate_task`, `execute_code`; they still come from `load_builtins` only, so no learning tools).
- Dependency: `jsonschema==4.26.0` (G, P10-04). Re-locked with `uv lock` under the unchanged `exclude-newer = 2026-10-04` (lock date 2026-10-18 kept); `uv tree` diff = only additions: jsonschema 4.26.0, attrs 26.1.0, jsonschema-specifications 2025.9.1, referencing 0.37.0, rpds-py 2026.6.3 (all MIT, all in THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md); no other version moved. `uv sync --frozen --all-extras` clean.
- Kernel/teardown wiring (one naming): the owner of a child's kernels is H's `kernel.child_owner(parent, child)`; G's `teardown.child_kernel_key` removed. `core.subagents.teardown` registers the built-in `code-kernels` releaser (`kernel.dispose_child`, local and remote pools). New core seam `SessionRuntime.code_kernel_owner` (set by `build_child`), offered by `tool_round.handler_extras` as `code_kernel_owner`. `_stubs_g.py` deleted; `test_teardown` drives the real process pool with stub kernels; no `# STAND-IN` file remains.
- Host lifecycle (new `service/code_host.py`): `SessionService.close` disposes the closed session's kernels (current id plus the ids it continued from after compression) — this is the `/new`, `/clear`, session-switch and exit path; `SessionService(kernels=KernelUpkeep(...))` starts `kernel.run_reaper` with the first attached session (interactive CLI only) and `aclose` shuts both pools down (before `shutdown_terminal`, so remote kernels still have their environments). `cli/runtime.build_service` passes it for every CLI run (`reaper` only when `run_context == "interactive"`). Gateway/TUI/ACP hosts (P15/P12/P19) pass their own `KernelUpkeep` once per process.
- Learning × delegation (gap found and fixed): a child reused `parent.options.builder`, which on the CLI is P09's learning stack — a subagent's prompt carried the parent's frozen memory, skills index and `-s` preloads. New `AgentOptions.subagent_builder` (the builder under the session-level wrappers), set by `cli/runtime.with_learning`, used by `build_child`. Children never review: they are built in core and never pass `agent_factory` (no `attach_review`, `before_turn`, `after_turn`, note sources), and `SessionLearning.attach_review` now forces `no_review` at `delegation_depth > 0`. Curator runs only from the CLI start check and the service maintenance timer; children are not service sessions. Pinned by `tests/integration/core/test_subagent_host.py` (also a child's `execute_code` end to end through `indus -z`: owner `<parent>::child::<child>`, pool empty after teardown, staging dir empty).
- Test fix at landing: `test_kernel.py::test_kernel_killed_between_calls_reports_lost_state` waited for the leader to show "Z" in `/proc`, which a multi-threaded runner does before `waitpid` can reap it, so the pool still saw it alive (4/10 failures alone); it now waits for `child.alive()` to turn false (15/15 alone).
- Intended differences recorded: D-16.160 … D-16.169, §2.7 (+ §3.1 row). D-16.160 is H's E9 rule: an interactive session is asked once per session (once / session / deny, never "always"); unattended runs (no broker, cron, `-z`, unattended) follow `approvals.*_mode` (deny by default) — `indus -z` refuses `execute_code` unless `approvals.single_query_mode: approve`.
- Open seams / follow-ups: (G) no `subagent.*` protocol events yet (TUI `/agents` work); the hand-off note is kept on the record, not shown in completion text; Docker container aliasing for children (children use their own task id on the parent's registry); per-group independent completions; durable background ledger. (H) public `Environment.temp_dir` (remote reads it when present); sweep of crashed-host staging dirs (`indus-kernel-*`/`indus-code-*` > 7 days); move `RemoteShell` to `tests/fakes/backend/`. G's background results now start their turn **through `inject`** (one host-initiated path, D-16.183): only while idle, so a message the person queued goes first; when not idle they stay queued and the next turn end retries.
- SECURITY (fixed at landing, D-16.171): the browser checked only the addresses a tool sent it to and where the page landed; a page's own requests (images, frames, `fetch`, forms, WebSockets) and redirect hops reached private and metadata addresses. New `tools/builtin/browser/guard.py`: `SessionManager` installs a `RequestGuard` on every session before first use (`BrowserHandle.guard(gate)`, gate = `url_policy.check_url_async` under the session scope, honouring `security.allow_private_urls`; a browser whose guard cannot be installed is closed). Route on the whole context of a launched browser (service workers blocked) or on our own tab of an attached one; refused requests aborted unsent; allowed ones sent by Playwright with `max_redirects=0`; sub-resource chains followed hop by hop in the guard (page gets the last answer); navigation chains walked (`HEAD`, GET on 405/501) and checked before the browser gets the first redirect; WebSockets checked before connecting; non-network schemes pass, other schemes refused; any guard error aborts. A proxy in the browser's passthrough env is handed to Playwright (`launch_proxy`), since the guard's fetches go out through Playwright. Tests: `tests/unit/tools/builtin/browser/test_guard.py` (17, fakes: route, redirects, hop limit, fail closed, sockets, manager/tool path, allow_private_urls, attach mode, failed install, proxy) and `tests/integration/tools/test_browser_ssrf.py` (real Chromium, marker `browser`: page embeds/redirects/fetches/sockets/navigates into a refused `127.0.0.1` server — zero hits; the test fails with the guard removed).
- Dependencies: extras `browser = ["playwright==1.63.0"]` and `voice = ["faster-whisper==1.2.1"]`. Re-locked with `uv lock` under the unchanged `exclude-newer = 2026-10-04`; package diff = only additions (av 19.0.1, ctranslate2 4.8.2, faster-whisper 1.2.1, filelock 4.0.10, flatbuffers 25.12.19, fsspec 2026.9.0, hf-xet 1.6.0, huggingface-hub 1.33.0, numpy 2.5.3, onnxruntime 1.30.0, protobuf 7.36.2, pyyaml 6.0.3, tokenizers 0.23.2, tqdm 4.70.1); playwright 1.63.0, greenlet 3.5.6, pyee 13.0.1 were **already in main's uv.lock** although main's `pyproject.toml` had no `browser` extra (the G+H re-lock was made with I's pyproject present — main's lock did not match its pyproject; now consistent). `uv sync --frozen --all-extras` clean; `uv tree` diff only additions. THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES rows for all 17. Pillow **not** added.
