## P13 wave 2 N1+N2 landing notes (lead) — P13-05..08 MCP discovery/calls/trust/reload, P13-09..12 OAuth, sampling+elicitation, OSV, `indus mcp`
- Order applied on ad24cb5 (P13 wave 1 landed): N1 then N2, each `git apply --3way`. Only conflict: `docs/internals/traps-ledger.md` R07.22 / R07.24 (union of both slices' test lines). Clean merges, checked by hand: config schema + its test (`approvals.mcp_reload_confirm`, `mcp.osv_preflight`), `providers/auxiliary.py` `call_route(temperature=, tools=)` (one implementation), `foundation/errors.py` codes, `cli/app.py` `mcp` row, non-TTY table and help catalogue, ACC-G14-1/G19-1. No dependency change, no re-lock. No `# STAND-IN` file existed or remains.
- Wiring (all through `SessionService(mcp=...)`, new `service/mcp_host.py` `McpUpkeep`): `cli/runtime.build_service` makes **one tool registry per run** (`learning_host.tool_registry`, shared by every agent through `AgentOptions.registry`) and an `McpUpkeep(scope, registry, aux=<the run's AuxClient>, watch=<interactive>)`; the TUI bridge host does the same (built-ins registry, watch on). The service awaits `upkeep.start()` before the first agent: when `mcp_servers` is non-empty it builds `McpHost(scope, registry, opener=extras.opener() (OSV-guarded), credential_stamp=oauth.credential_stamp(scope), extras=…)`, `register_host`, `await host.discover()`, starts the `ConfigWatcher` (interactive), and `aclose` stops the watcher, closes the servers and drops the host. A profile without servers never imports the MCP stack (~0.7 s). Per server, `ServerHandle` calls the host's extras factory → `extras_for(name, server, scope, base=host.options, sampling=aux_backend(aux))`: its options (OAuth handler, sampling/elicitation kwargs) go to `ManagedServer`, its `Requesters` to the handle; `calls.call` wraps each `tools/call` in `requesters.serving(broker, unattended=<no broker or cron/single_query/unattended>, turn_id=…)`. A restarted server gets new extras; an explicit `/reload-mcp` (`reconcile(reason="reload")`) also restarts a **parked** server so its OAuth handler is rebuilt. P13-07's parking keys on `McpAuthRequired` too (`trust.is_auth_failure` walks the cause chain); the stamp is a hash of the stored token record, so a login in another process un-parks at the next discovery/reload.
- Tool surface: discovery also maintains the umbrella alias **`mcp`** → every `mcp-<server>`; `McpUpkeep.selection` adds it to a platform bundle selection (`None` = all already includes them). `ToolSurfaceChanged` → every attached agent's new `Agent.tools_changed(selection)`: the stored tool array and prompt stay byte-identical until the next compaction, where the new core seam **`SessionRuntime.on_compaction`** (run by `compression.engine._apply` between `prompts.invalidate` and the rebuild) re-selects the tools and rebuilds `runtime.tools` and `prompt_context.tool_names`; new sessions get the new tools at once. The provider prompt cache cannot be dropped client-side; the rebuild is the planned miss (logged at info).
- Commands: `cli/slash/mcp_cmds.py` — `/mcp` (N2's `status_rows(scope, live=…)` / `status_lines` with live states and a detail line per server) and `/reload-mcp` (yes/no on the REPL thread while `approvals.mcp_reload_confirm` is on, refuses without a person, then `upkeep.reload()` on the service loop via `upkeep.call`); both import the MCP stack lazily. `/mcp` is `busy="dispatch"`. Registry `landed_commands` gains `mcp`, `reload-mcp`; help group `tools` gains them; `/re` ambiguity golden gains `/reload-mcp`, `/reload_mcp`; the "not built yet" examples moved to `/agents`. TUI bridge `methods_config.MCP_RELOAD = "indus.mcp.reload:reload"` and `reloaded` now comes from the outcome. `indus chat` shows `DiscoveryReport.summary()` as a start notice when a server failed, is misconfigured or cooling down.
- SECURITY (fixed at landing, D-16.185): a server could write `MEDIA:/path/of/my/key` in its text and have the agent hand a local file to a delivery scanner. `calls.neutral` defuses every `MEDIA:` directive (any case, spaces, full-width colon) in text and resource blocks, `isError` text and JSON-RPC error messages, `structuredContent`, `_meta` and the utility rows; the joined text is neutralised as a whole (a directive split across blocks is caught) while the call's own saved-file lines carry a one-off marker and become `MEDIA:` afterwards. Tests: `test_calls.py::test_media_directives_in_server_text_are_neutralised`, `::test_media_directives_in_errors_and_utilities_are_neutralised`, and through a real stdio server and the fake model in `tests/integration/service/test_mcp_host.py`. P15's delivery scanner must still deliver trusted paths only (the model can write a directive itself).
- OAuth (fixed at landing, D-16.188): `oauth.login` snapshots every stored entry of the server (`OAuthStore.snapshot`), clears the tokens for the login and restores the snapshot when the login raises, is cancelled or ends without tokens (a half-made dynamic registration is rolled back too). `login` / `device_login` refuse outside `trust.allow_interactive_auth()`; `indus mcp login` runs inside it. Tests: `test_oauth.py::test_a_failed_login_leaves_the_previous_sign_in_in_place`, `::test_a_login_is_refused_outside_a_persons_own_sign_in`.
- New tests: `tests/integration/service/test_mcp_host.py` (real CLI host: discovery before the first agent, `indus-cli` + `mcp`, model → stdio server call, `/mcp`, `/reload-mcp` cancel / no person / yes, stored array unchanged until `on_compaction`, new session gets the new server, host exit closes servers and unregisters; a profile without servers starts no host), `test_calls.py::test_a_call_runs_inside_the_servers_requester_slot`, `test_trust.py::test_a_background_sign_in_refusal_parks_like_a_401`, `::test_reload_restarts_a_parked_server_with_fresh_extras`, `tests/unit/core/test_agent_p08.py::test_a_tool_change_waits_for_the_next_compaction` (the `on_compaction` seam through a real `agent.compress()`).
- Intended differences recorded: D-16.184 … D-16.191, §2.7 (+ §3.1 row).
- Open seams: (N1) the tool-search bridge (C4) should mark `mcp__` tools deferrable; `-t/--toolsets` does not filter which servers start (every enabled server starts, and the `mcp` umbrella is added to an explicit `-t` selection too); a non-boolean `readOnlyHint` makes the SDK reject the whole listing; no schema-cache expiry (`ttlMs` ignored). (N2) drop a dead dynamic registration on `invalid_client`; guard the SDK's guessed `/register` endpoint; no CIMD document of our own; server-side sampling is deprecated from protocol 2026-07-28 (client side kept). (Lead) a profile with no servers at start has no config watcher (`/reload-mcp` starts them); sampling usage goes through the host's aux client, not a session ledger; with two sessions calling one server at once the latest call's session answers elicitations; the gateway (P15) and ACP (P19) hosts must pass their own `McpUpkeep`; plugin `call_mcp` can use `calls.call(host.handles[name], spec, args, CallContext(...))`.
- Gate (this landing, verify tree on ad24cb5 + N1 + N2 + integration): ruff check/format, pyright (0 errors), lint-imports (1 kept) clean; `INDUS_STRESS_SECONDS=10 pytest -m "not perf"` 9674 passed, 9 skipped, 11 deselected (first run, no failures; `test_agent_p08.py::test_a_tool_change_waits_for_the_next_compaction` was added after it and passes with its file, 8 passed); `pytest -m perf` alone 11 passed; `tooling.checks --require-ref` ok; `CI=true pnpm test --maxWorkers=1` 855 passed (58 files); staged diff has no credential-shaped literal; no writes under `/root`, no stray processes or `/tmp` files.
## P13 wave 2 N3+N4 landing notes (lead) — P13-18 prompt sections, P13-19 shell hooks + `indus hooks`, P13-21..23 plugin install/consent/CLI, `pre_llm_call`; P13-25..28 skills hub and project-skill trust
- Order applied on e074c01 (N1+N2 landed): N3 then N4, each `git apply --3way` from ad24cb5. Conflicts (N3 only), all unions: `cli/app.py` (`mcp`, `hooks`, `plugins` rows), `core/turn_state.py` (N1's `on_compaction` beside N3's `llm_context`; `agent.py`, `prologue.py`, `prompt/builder.py` merged clean — `tools_changed` / `on_compaction` kept), non-TTY table (`mcp`, `hooks`, `plugins` rows; N4's `skills` hub rows merged clean), help catalogue. Config schema + test merged clean (`plugins.clone_timeout_seconds` / `auto_update_check_hours` / `auto_apply`, top-level `hooks`, `hooks_auto_accept`, `skills.hub.*`, `skills.trusted_project_dirs`, `skills.project_discovery`). No dependency change, no re-lock. No `# STAND-IN` file existed or remains.
- SECURITY (fixed at landing, D-16.192): `safety.content_scan` (used by the hub, plugin install and project skills) rated `` `chmod -R 777 /etc` `` `safe` while the bare text was `caution`; a fence closing on the command's line, indentation (`env | curl …`), quotes, `<code>` tags and a closing full stop hid others the same way (pattern ends at whitespace/shell punctuation, start-of-command anchors). Each line is now also matched as its code-span / `<code>` / quote contents and as a markup-free, indentation-free reading (`_command_views`); views only add findings; the negatable prose rule (`approval_bypass`) still reads the whole line. Tests: `test_content_scan.py` (108-case span/fence/quote matrix + 4 focused tests; 4 corpus positives, 3 negatives), `hub/test_install.py::test_commands_in_code_spans_and_fences_are_not_hidden_from_the_policy`, `test_project_trust.py::test_a_command_in_a_code_span_or_fence_is_quarantined` (all fail on the old scanner). Cost: ~2× on lines with markup (1 MB of such lines 2.3 s → 4.8 s).
- Wiring (new `service/plugin_host.py` `PluginUpkeep`, one per session host like `McpUpkeep`): `SessionService(plugins=…)` awaits `start()` before the first agent — `HookBus.from_scope`, `PluginHost` over the run's shared registry (ports: only `reserved_commands` = the slash registry), `discover` + `PluginLoader.load`, then `shell_hooks.register_shell_hooks(bus, scope, interactive=, surface=, accept=, ask=, cwd=)`; a profile with no `plugins.enabled` and no `hooks` imports nothing. Per top-level session: `selection()` adds the toolsets loaded plugins registered tools into; `options()` applies `plugins.session.plugin_options` only when something is live (empty profile: options untouched, goldens unchanged), with the `SessionPrompts` prompt store so frozen sections restore on resume; `on_session_start` after attach, `on_session_end` before close; `aclose` undoes every plugin and drops the shell hooks. Subagents: `subagent_builder` stays the builder under the sections, `llm_context` is never passed to children (they keep the tool guards through `ChildHooks`). `cli/runtime.build_service` passes `PluginUpkeep(surface="cli", interactive=<run_context interactive>, accept=spec.accept_hooks, ask=<click confirm only on a TTY>)`; the TUI bridge host passes `surface="tui"` (no consent question there yet). `--accept-hooks` global flag → `GlobalArgs.accept_hooks` → `ServiceSpec.accept_hooks` (chat and `-z`). `/plugins [list|enable|disable <name>]` (`cli/slash/plugin_cmds.py`; registry row now landed, help group `tools`, catalogue text updated) and the running host's plugin slash commands through `Extensions.plugin_commands` (live view; runs on the service loop). The interactive host's maintenance timer gains `plugin updates` (`plugin_host.update_check`: `Installer.maybe_check_updates()` only when something is installed; first tick after 90 s, never at start). `toggle_plugin` now says the change applies at the next start (plugins load once per host).
- Gate fixes at landing: the lookup ratchet caught two raw `shutil.which` calls (N3 `cli/commands/hooks.py` doctor, N4 hub advisory scanner) — both now use `platform.locate.which`. The new integration tests run `indus -z`, whose exit shuts the process terminal runtime down and raises the process-wide foreground fence; `test_plugin_host.py` resets it after each test (as `tests/unit/cli/conftest.py` does), otherwise the next spawn in the same interpreter (`test_terminal_local.py`) got rc 130.
- N4 wiring was already in the slice: project-skill trust question + banner row in `cli/launch.py`, `/skills search|install` in `learning_cmds.py`, P09 helpers write real hub lock entries; P09 curator / bundled sync / journey / usage suites pass against the real lock.
- New tests: `tests/integration/service/test_plugin_host.py` (real CLI host: plugin tool in the selection, frozen section in the system prompt and byte-identical on the next request, plugin + shell `pre_llm_call` context on the user message only, shell `pre_tool_call` blocks a `curl` terminal call, session start/end hooks, `/plugins`, a plugin slash command, registrations taken back at close; a finite run skips unconsented hooks and asks nothing; a bare profile loads nothing; `indus -z --accept-hooks`; the P13 Done-when demo `indus plugins install <dir> --enable` → tool + `pre_tool_call` curl guard through `indus -z`), `tests/unit/service/test_plugin_host.py` (update check, selection, `/plugins`).
- Intended differences recorded: D-16.192 … D-16.204, §2.7 (+ §3.1 row).
- Open seams: skills.sh API shape to be verified against its docs before 1.0 (N4 took it from reference behaviour); packaging rule for `optional-skills/` (`indus/_official_skills`) once the owner adds content; P14 cron must wrap runs in `project_trust.bind_project_cwd(job.workdir)` and build its own `PluginUpkeep` (non-interactive) if cron sessions should run hooks; P15 gateway buttons for `/skills install` and its own `PluginUpkeep` (+ consent card); P20 desktop deep link `skills_hub.deep_link_install(scope, url, confirm_install=…)`; TUI shell-hook consent question and `--accept-hooks` forwarding (today: env var / config only); plugin host ports not wired yet: `mcp_call` (use `calls.call(host.handles[name], …)`), `llm_complete` (aux client), `inject` / `post`; plugin `register_cli_command` not added to `indus`; plugin skills not fed to the P09 resolver; memory provider manager still not in the session host; plugins load once per host (enable/disable applies at the next start, no reload); `pre_verify` and other observer hooks (`post_llm_call`, `on_session_finalize`, …) still have no call sites.
- P13 "Done when": met — all suites green (see gate), golden prompt files unchanged (sections/hooks add nothing to an empty profile), `tooling.checks --require-ref` ok; demo pieces: `indus mcp add` + prefixed callable tools + untrusted asks (N1/N2), `indus plugins install <dir> --enable` adds a tool and a curl-blocking `pre_tool_call` hook through `indus -z` (new integration test; a hung `pre_tool_call` blocks after the 30 s hook deadline — `test_shell_hooks.py::test_timeout_blocks_and_kills_the_group`, D-04.1 (d) and ACC-G3-1), `indus skills install <url>` refuses a dangerous skill even with `--force` (`hub/test_install.py::test_dangerous_never_installs`). No `fixtures/hello-plugin` folder is committed; the demo plugin is written by the test.
- Gate (this landing, verify tree on e074c01 + N3 + N4 + integration): ruff check/format, pyright (0 errors), lint-imports (1 kept) clean; `INDUS_STRESS_SECONDS=10 pytest -m "not perf"` run 4: 10040 passed, 9 skipped, 11 deselected (run 1 found the two `shutil.which` ratchet hits and the foreground-fence leak above, both fixed; runs 2–3 each failed one different load-sensitive test untouched by this landing — `ssh/test_environment.py::test_home_detection_retried_no_sooner_than_30s`, `memory/test_providers.py::test_slow_prefetch_bounded_by_eight_seconds` — each 5/5 alone); `pytest -m perf` alone 11 passed; `tooling.checks --require-ref` ok; `CI=true pnpm test --maxWorkers=1` 855 passed (58 files); staged diff has no credential-shaped literal; no writes under `/root`, no stray processes or test files in `/tmp` (only uv/pnpm tool locks).
## P14 landing notes (lead) — P14-01..19 cron (slices C1, C2, C3)
- Order applied on bde6067 (P13 wave 2 N1+N2 landed): C1, C2, C3, each `git apply --3way` from adaceac. Conflicts: `cron/ports.py` (C2 and C3 both added it — merged into one module: C2's `JobSpec`/`spec_of`/`CronPorts` for the ticker and runner, C3's `CronBackend`/`field` helpers for the surfaces; **one way in: `ports.BACKEND_FACTORY`**, C2's `set_ports_factory`/`ports_for` removed — the backend builds the ticker's `CronPorts`); `foundation/config/schema.py` (three `CronSection` classes → one, C1+C2+C3 rows, `allow_agent_scheduling` once; two `cron:` fields → one) + its test; traps ledger R07.13 (C1+C2 lines), R07.15 (C2+C3); acceptance map ACC-H3-1/H3-2; `test_registry.py` (`mcp`, `reload-mcp`, `cron` landed; tools help group). `tools/toolsets.py`, `cli/runtime.py`, `session_service.py`, `dispatch.py`, threat scan merged clean.
- Dependency: `croniter==6.2.4` (C1). Re-locked with `uv lock` under the unchanged `exclude-newer = 2026-10-04`; `uv tree` diff = only additions: croniter 6.2.4, python-dateutil 2.9.0.post0, six 1.17.0 (MIT / Apache-2.0 OR BSD-3-Clause / MIT; THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES rows; layout §4 pin row was already there). `uv sync --frozen --all-extras` clean.
- Wiring: **`service.cron_host.CronHost`** is the one backend (the C3 `CronBackend` and the source of the C2 `CronPorts`): C1's store (with C2's script rule, `context.script_rule`), ledger, notepad and health under C2's ticker; `install()` sets `ports.BACKEND_FACTORY` once (at import of `cron_host`; `indus cron`, `/cron` and the CLI tool registry import it). The ledger database opens per use (`CronHost.opened()`), never held between calls. Ports: due = C1's `evaluate_all`/`collect_due` (non-firing repairs patched; a firing decision's `next_run_at` + `last_dispatch` written **with the claim** — `DueSlot.updates`, `ticker._take_claim(updates=…)`); a fire at `unreachable_retry.at` is a `kind="retry"` attempt (`bookkeeping.is_retry_fire`); claims stored as `FireClaim` models (ticker reads them as mappings); `finish` = failure → `incidents.decide_failure_alert` (only when the outcome delivers and bookkeeping would notify) → `delivery.deliver` (+ nudge + hold line) → `bookkeeping.settle_run` **inside one locked store write** with the store's planner (`plan=` → `plan_next` after `last_run_at`/`repeat`, from the slot; a manual run of a recurring job keeps its schedule; the ticker's claim is never cleared by bookkeeping) → success resolves incidents; the delivery status goes onto the ledger attempt (`LedgerPort.note_delivery`, settle passes the outcome's error too). `PreflightProbes.mcp` reads `mcp.discovery.host_for(scope).status()`; preflight targets read with `cron.targets`; `monitor.RUN_SCRIPT` = the cron script runner; `tool._SCAN_SCOPE = "cron"`; doctor reads C1's `DispatchStamp` (`lateness`, `lateness_s`). `CronJob` gains `unreachable_retry {attempt, at, expr}`; `last_delivery_unverified` is a list. `cron.*` codes are rows of `foundation.errors.ERROR_CODES` (`cron.errors.CRON_ERROR_CODES` is a view of them). Curator protection: `InProcessHost.start` registers `register_cron_references(CronStore(scope))` (undone at stop); the CLI start curator check and `indus curator run` wrap their pass in `cron_refs.cron_references(scope)`. The `cronjob_manage` result sink is registered again under the new id when a rotating compaction moves a session (C3 follow-up closed).
- `indus-cron` bundle = core minus `clarify` and `cronjob_manage`; the runner adds the `cronjob` toolset only under `cron.allow_agent_scheduling` (and still denies the tool by name otherwise). Goldens: the cron surface `tools.json` comes from `load_builtins` (no `cronjob_manage`); `--update-golden` run, no drift.
- CLI: `cron` in `cli/app.py` REGISTRY (after `mcp`), help-order golden, non-TTY rows for every verb plus three `with_arguments` rows. **`cron daemon` decision:** the non-TTY table gains a `long_running:` list — its commands must have a row there instead of an exit code, and the contract runs only `<command> --help` (exit 0 within the 5 s watchdog). `indus cron tick` prints `<job> <status>` per run (the e2e test reads it).
- Stand-ins removed: `tests/unit/cron/_stubs_c2.py`, `_stubs_c3.py`, `tests/integration/cron/tick_entry.py`. Their tests now run on the real store: `tests/unit/cron/world.py` (`make_world(scope)` — the host's real ports with seams for health, reap counting, finish recording and monitor answers; `record(...)`; `RecordingHost(CronHost)` for the CLI/tool tests), closed by `tests/unit/cron/conftest.py`. `test_tick_e2e` creates the job with `indus cron create` and runs `python -m indus cron tick`. No `# STAND-IN` file remains.
- Behaviour notes from the real store: an interval steps from the **end** of the last run (C1, drift kept) — the C2 daemon test now allows the second slot up to a minute after the hour; a manual run does not move a recurring job's schedule (as the CLI help says).
- New tests: `tests/integration/cron/test_m4_demo.py` (the "Done when" demo: `/cron add "every 30m" check the build`, `cronjob_manage` create, `indus cron create` with a missing key; ticks on the fake clock fire them through the real runner and fake model servers; output documents, local delivery recorded on the attempt, `[SILENT]` not delivered, `[JOB_FAILED]` delivered once with streak 1→3 and one alerted incident, success resolves it; the missing key is blocked once with no model call; pause → resume and 3 h downtime each catch up once; an attempt whose process died is `unknown` after reaping and its slot never runs; `status`/`list`; plus the late run in the repeated fall-back hour, end to end, which fails without the bookkeeping planner), `tests/unit/service/test_cron_host.py` (install once, host start protects cron skills, MCP probe, target preflight), `test_session_service_p08.py` (sink follows the rotation), `test_monitor.py::test_script_monitor_uses_the_cron_script_runner`, `test_ticker.py::test_take_claim_writes_the_due_rules_updates`.
- Rebased onto 7867bdf (P13 N3+N4 landed). Conflicts, all unions: `cli/app.py` (`hooks`, `plugins`, then `cron`), `cli/runtime.py` (P13's `HookSpec` type import kept), `session_service.py` (P13's `plugins.session_started` and the cron result sink both kept at attach), non-TTY table (hooks/plugins rows, cron rows, the `long_running` section), help-order golden (`hooks`, `plugins`, `cron`), `test_registry.py` (`plugins` and `cron` landed, tools help group), 16 §2.7 (D-16.192 … D-16.204 and D-16.220 … D-16.237) and §3.1, LEAD-NOTES (both sections).
- Project-skill trust (P13-28 seam closed): the cron host wraps **every run** in `project_trust.bind_project_cwd(job.workdir)` through the new `RunEnv.around` hook (preflight's skill check, skill loading for the prompt and the agent run all read the job's project; no workdir → the bound folder is cleared for the run), and `CronAgentRunner` binds it around its own run too. Tests: `tests/unit/service/test_cron_host.py::test_a_run_reads_project_skills_from_the_job_workdir`, `::test_the_agent_runner_binds_the_workdir_itself`.
- `PluginUpkeep` for cron: **not added.** P13's notes make it conditional ("if cron sessions should run hooks") and no P14 row asks for plugin tools, hooks or prompt sections in scheduled runs; a cron session therefore has no plugin tools, sections or `pre_llm_call`/shell hooks. Decide when the ticker moves into the gateway (P15-21), which owns its own `PluginUpkeep`.
- Intended differences recorded: D-16.220 … D-16.237, §2.7 (+ §3.1 row).
- Open seams: no `PluginUpkeep` in cron runs (above); skill **bundles** in a job's skills are not expanded; a job's **MCP servers are not merged into an explicit toolset list** (and the cron process starts no MCP host, so the MCP preflight is skipped there); a **manual run of a paused job** runs without resuming it (kept; confirm); platform delivery and `all` are blocked until P15 (deliverers register through `delivery.register_deliverer`); store-health notices are logged only (no delivered notice); the incident table keeps its own SQLite connection; when the open incident holds a repeated failure, the third run's review nudge is held with it (shows on the reminder); `cron.toolsets`/job toolsets naming `cronjob` still get the tool denied without the opt-in.
## P15 wave 1 G1–G4 landing notes (lead) — P15-01..05 fake platform, contract suite, config, events, send/retry, adapter base; P15-06..10 registry/token locks, session keys + routes, authz, pairing + `indus pairing`, bot-loop/concurrency/slash tiers; P15-14..18 chunker, formatters, stream consumer, sender, media policy; P15-11..13 admission, busy guards, chat commands
- Order applied on 6d422e7 (P13 done): G1, G2, G3 (`git apply --3way` from 8f99060), then G4 (from 0513340 = 8f99060 + G1 + G2 raw). Conflicts, all unions: config schema (`hooks` / `hooks_auto_accept` beside `platforms` / `gateway`), `cli/app.py` (`hooks`, `plugins`, `pairing` rows), `gateway/core/__init__.py` (one docstring), ACC-K4-1, ACC-K29-1, R06.13, R06.24 (both slices' tests; the stale "Review:" lines dropped). G4 merged clean (registry chat-only rows `bg`/`background`, `sethome`, `platform`, `verbose`, `personality`, `commands`; 6 locale lines; `indus-telegram/discord/slack` toolset bundles). No dependency change, no re-lock.
- Stand-ins removed (none left with the `# STAND-IN` first line): `_stubs_g2.py` → `tests/unit/gateway/builders.py` (real `Source` via `src()` / `replace()`; `PolicyAdapter` on the real base with per-instance capabilities); `_stubs_g3.py` → `tests/unit/gateway/outbound/recording.py` (a recording adapter on the real base: ops at the public door with the caller's marks, `wire_meta` at the hooks; failures are real `SendResult.failure` texts checked against the classifier); `_stubs_g4.py` → the real reply port. Model changes for the wiring: `Source.role_authorized`; `Adapter.access_policy(kind)` / `allows_dm(user_id)` / `resolved_allowlist_ids()` (safe defaults); `Capabilities.markup` (and `Adapter.format` = `formatter_for(markup)`); `code_points` / `utf16_units` / `LenFn` now one copy (chunker's); media methods take `reply_to` (hooks read `meta["reply_to"]`); the base strips `interim` / `final` before every hook and a `final` mark on an edit makes it a final edit; `SendResult.skip()` is `ok=True, skipped=True`. `_shapes.py` keeps the core free of an adapters import but is now typed to the real shapes (`AdapterLike.capabilities.*` + methods; no `getattr`). `ports.py` calls the real `Adapter`, `SendResult` (`chat_unreachable` = `SendResult.chat_unreachable`, i.e. G1's chat-level not-found reading), `SentIndex.record(platform, …)`; an unsupported media kind falls back to a document.
- Fixed at landing: `PlatformRegistry.load_plugin_entries` read `plugin_id` from P13's `ProviderEntry`, whose field is `plugin` (plugin platforms would have lost their plugin name); new `test_registry.py::test_a_real_plugin_host_slot_feeds_the_registry` goes through a real `PluginHost`.
- LAYER RULE (lead, D-16.246): no `ignore_imports` exemption in the import-linter contract. What two top-layer siblings (cli, gateway, tui_bridge, web, acp, …) share lives below them in `indus.state` or `indus.service`. The pairing store moved to `indus.state.pairing` (`indus pairing` and the gateway share it; `allowlist_env_name` = the registry's `INDUS_<PLATFORM>_ALLOWED_USERS` rule, pinned by a test); the routes index already lives in `state/gateway_routes.py`. **P15-28's `indus gateway` commands** must follow it: status-file reader, planned-stop record, unit/plist generators and the control-socket client go in `service/` (or `state/` for stored records), and the `run` entry point is registered by `"module:attr"` string in `cli/app.py` like `tui-bridge` (no `from indus.gateway` inside `indus.cli`).
- Config (one `GatewaySection`, all defaults from the shared table, R06.25): `gateway.trust_env` true, `group_sessions_per_user` true, `thread_sessions_per_user` false, `unauthorized_dm_behavior` pair, `bot_loop_guard {enabled true, max_messages 20, window_seconds 300, cooldown_seconds 600}`, `max_concurrent_sessions` none, `media_strict` false, `media_allow_dirs` [], **`media_trust_recent` false** (lead decision, D-16.249: Linux has no birth time, `touch` fakes recency), `media_trust_recent_seconds` 600; `platforms.<name>.streaming` / `tool_progress` (unset = platform default; `PlatformSettings.streaming` / `.tool_progress`); `display.ephemeral_system_ttl` 0, `tool_progress_grouping` accumulate, `heartbeat_seconds` 180 (`GatewaySettings.display`); `approvals.destructive_slash_confirm` false (also read by P11 `/rollback`). Readers: `BotLoopSettings` and `build_session_key` defaults come from the schema; `session_keys.key_options(section)`; `MediaPolicy.for_scope(scope)` reads the profile's section (`gateway.config.gateway_section`). Tests: `test_schema.py` pins + `test_gateway_rows_validate`, `test_config.py::test_gateway_rows_reach_their_readers`, `::test_streaming_and_tool_progress_follow_platform_defaults`, `test_media_policy.py::test_policy_reads_the_profiles_gateway_section`.
- Plugins: `"platform"` added to P13 `PROVIDER_KINDS`. No gateway host exists yet: **P15-24's daemon** builds its own `PluginUpkeep(surface="gateway", interactive=False)`, awaits `start()`, then calls `PlatformRegistry.load_plugin_entries(upkeep.host.providers)` before the enablement pass (and passes its own `McpUpkeep`, per the P13 notes).
- Session deletion drops gateway routes (G2 follow-up): `state/deletion.py` deletes `gateway_routes` rows whose `session_id` is deleted and clears `prev_session_id` pointing at it; the chat's next message gets a fresh session (`test_gateway_routes.py::test_deleting_a_session_drops_its_routes`).
- G4 reply port: `gateway/outbound/replies.py` `PlatformReplies(senders)` implements `ReplySender.reply(source, text, *, reply_to, kind)` over one `OutboundSender` per platform (`final` / `command` = a reply; `notice` without a message to reply to = unsolicited, dead targets skipped; `ack` = ephemeral, D-16.250). All 70 G4 tests now run through the real sender (formatting, redaction, silence tokens, sent index).
- Intended differences recorded: D-16.238 … D-16.253, §2.7 (+ §3.1 row).
- Open seams: (G4) admission is not wired into a daemon yet — P15-24 builds `AdmissionPipeline(...)` + `start()` (adapter sinks → accept; turn-end / session-moved / input-probe listeners; `hooks=HookBus`, `automation=SessionAutomation`, `cap=ConcurrencyCap(lambda: section.max_concurrent_sessions)`, `bot_guard=BotLoopGuard(settings_reader(view.get))`, `global_unknown_dm=lambda: section.unauthorized_dm_behavior`, `build_session_key(**key_options(section))`, `PlatformReplies` with `OutboundSender(reply_to_mode=platform.reply_to_mode, ephemeral_ttl=display.ephemeral_system_ttl, sent_index=…, dead_targets=DeadTargets.for_scope(scope), media=MediaPolicy.for_scope(scope, section))`; the runner feeds `StreamConsumer` / `ToolProgress` / `TypingLoop` / `Heartbeat(interval=display.heartbeat_seconds)` and calls `deliver` only when the stream did not deliver the final); P15-22 approvals through `on_request=` / `interceptors=`, P15-23 media through `enrich=`; `ChatCommands(plugin_commands=, command_hooks=)`; G4's `identity_env()` names blocked by `child_env`; active-turn marker clear behind the P15-19 ledger write (R06.5); pending queue in memory. (G1–G3) `OutboundSender` does not use `Adapter.send_with_retry` yet (retries and the delivery ledger arrive with P15-19); `proxy_for(trust_env=section.trust_env)` and token-lock liveness (P15-24 replaces the private start-time helper); Slack `raw_text` table cell vs the pinned SDK, Discord link → id normalisation, Slack `typing_status_text` (P15-33..35, with the adapter SDK pins); Docker container-path translation for media (P11 backend); plugin platform blocks log "unknown setting (kept)" until P16; P15-19.. later tasks (ledger, runner, approvals UI, daemon, API server, webhooks, service install).
## P21 B1+B2 landing notes (lead) — P21-01..04, 11, 15 (B1), P21-05..10 (B2), P21-12-sized wiring
- Order applied on 2d0cbf9 (P00–P14 landed; base 730cf11): B1, then B2, each `git apply --3way`. Only conflict: `foundation/config/schema.py` terminal section (B1's `singularity_image`, `scratch_dir`, `disk_warning_gb` beside B2's `modal_mode`, `modal_image`, `daytona_image`, `vercel_runtime`, `sync_back_max_bytes`; union). `test_schema.py`, traps ledger and acceptance map merged clean (unions checked by hand).
- One adapter (new `backends/sdk_ports.py`): `VendorSupport` serves B2's `SdkSupport` (`call` → `sdk_call.sdk_call` on `SDK_POOL` with an `Operation` built from the op name, timeout and idempotence, `on_deadline` → the wrapper's one-shot cancel; `guard` → `guarded_command`; `stage_stdin` / `stdin_chunks` → `stdin_payload`), `ProfileRecords` serves `SandboxRecords` over `sandbox_records.SandboxRecords` through `run_blocking` on the `io` pool (deadline 30 s real time). The `SandboxRecords` port is **async** now (the store locks and fsyncs); the Modal/Vercel lifecycles await it, log a failed drop/save, and let a busy store fail the open (D-16.260). The stdin port gained `remove` (an incomplete upload is deleted). `sdk_shell` deadlines are the `sdk_call` constants (one source).
- Stand-in removed: `tests/unit/backends/_stubs_b2.py`. B2's tests run on B1's fakes through `tests/unit/backends/sdk_rig.py` (`RecordingSupport` = the real `VendorSupport` with a call log, the real records store on the test profile, a `Cloud` per test). The fakes gained what the real SDKs have and the tests need: `Cloud.hang_command(fragment)`; Modal's stdout reader is iterable (streams as the process writes, as the SDK's does); Daytona `download_file(remote, local)` (the SDK's second form); Vercel `workspace=` (the sandbox cwd under the cloud root), `status_script`, and `create_process` that returns while the command runs (`wait()` collects, `kill()` signals its group). The surface self-test is unchanged (only vendor names). No `# STAND-IN` file remains.
- P21-11 wiring: `vendor_env.register_vendor_names()` runs in `cli/entry.py` right after `register_blocked_names("providers", …)` (the gateway/TUI/ACP hosts that do not go through `cli/entry` must call it too — P15/P17/P19). The SDK flow hands `passthrough_plan` values to each vendor's exec env parameter (Modal `exec(env=)`, Daytona `process.exec(env=)`, Vercel `create_process(env=)`), unsets value-less names in the script and keeps every forwarded name out of the snapshot (`_excluded_names`); new test `test_daytona.py::test_forwarded_variables_reach_the_sandbox_through_the_sdk_env_only`.
- Registry (P21-12-sized): `default_entries` = local, docker, ssh, `singularity_entry()`, and the three rows of new `backends/sdk_entries.py` (`modal`, `daytona`, `vercel_sandbox`). Probes read the extra's distribution from package metadata (a missing extra is named, `indus-agent[<extra>]`) and then the backend's `requirement_reason`; no SDK import, no network. `BackendEntry.cleanup_bound_s` (new; `EnvironmentRegistry.cleanup_bound_for`) gives the SDK rows **195 s** (bulk sync-back 120 + snapshot 60 + stop 15) instead of 15 s. A persistent SDK sandbox is profile-scoped (task key `shared`, as Singularity). Factories pass `VendorSupport()` and `ProfileRecords.for_scope(scope, kind)` (kinds `modal`, `vercel`). `ERROR_CODES`: `backend.deadline`, `backend.sandbox_records_busy`, `backend.singularity.build`. New tests `tests/unit/backends/test_sdk_entries.py` (seven rows, flags equal to the environments', cleanup bounds, probes never import an SDK, factories hand the shared ports and task keys, records off the loop, `VendorSupport`).
- Dependencies: extras `modal = ["modal==1.6.1"]`, `daytona = ["daytona==0.220.0"]`, `vercel = ["vercel-sandbox==0.7.0"]`. Re-locked under the unchanged `exclude-newer = 2026-10-04`; package diff = **only additions, 56 packages, no version moved**; `uv sync --frozen --all-extras` clean. Licences checked by hand from the installed dist-info (the guard walks only `[project].dependencies`): all permissive — Apache-2.0, MIT, BSD-2/3-Clause, `aiohappyeyeballs` PSF-2.0, **`bidict` MPL-2.0** (allow-listed, unmodified, via `python-socketio`), `daytona-analytics-api-client(-async)` have no licence metadata but ship an Apache-2.0 LICENSE file. THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES rows for all 56 (the `opentelemetry-api` row now says an exporter comes with the `daytona` extra).
- Intended differences recorded: D-16.260 … D-16.270, §2.7 (+ §3.1 row; lead rulings Vercel poll 500 ms = D-16.266, stale sync-back temp only when the owner is dead and > 30 min = D-16.269).
- Left for later (recorded): **P21-13** doctor/setup entries (the setup picker now lists the four new rows because it reads `default_entries`, but asks for no credentials yet); **P21-14** per-task overrides (P22's `worker.ImagePrep.register` is where they plug in); **P21-16** the seven-backend contract suite over the fakes and the `network` live lane (Singularity already runs in the shared/shell contract rows); the rest of P21-12: flapping grace (R02.13) and the unscoped-probe re-probe (R02.29) are not built — the probes are pure lookups for now.
- Open seams: an SDK command holds one `SDK_POOL` worker for its whole run (8 workers, queue 64: at most 8 SDK commands at once per process); a hung vendor call keeps its worker until it returns; Vercel's runtime-in-image-slot and the OIDC-only path (token from the process env) need the live lane; Daytona's SDK turns on OpenTelemetry export when `DAYTONA_OTEL_ENABLED`/`DAYTONA_EXPERIMENTAL_OTEL_ENABLED=true` is in the environment (we do not pass `otel_enabled=False`); confirm the Vercel and Modal SDK imports send no telemetry (B2 follow-up); `scripts/dev/sdk_surfaces.py` is run by hand in a throwaway venv; the phase file's `all` extra does not exist; ACC-D5-2 (overlay restart) needs a real Apptainer.
## P22 R1 landing notes (lead) — P22-01..09 dataset, distributions, converter, stats, checkpoint, batch files, worker, orchestration, `indus research`
- Applied after P21 B1+B2 on the same tree (`git apply --3way`, base 730cf11): clean (`.gitignore` `!docs/user/reference/`, traps ledger R10.1–R10.7, `cli/app.py` `research` row, config `research.runs_root` + test, non-TTY table, help catalogue golden). No dependency change.
- Layering (lead decision): `indus.research` moved **one layer below the user surfaces** (between them and `indus.service`) in the import-linter contract (`pyproject.toml`), `tests/unit/test_layers.py` and 00-layout §1; `cli/commands/research.py` imports `indus.research.batch` / `.distributions` directly inside its verbs (the `importlib`-by-name helper is gone). P22-10's `--save-trajectories` can import the saver the same way. `lint-imports`: 1 kept.
- Kept: `worker.ImagePrep` with `register = refuse_images` by default — **P21-14 plugs its per-task override registry in there** (task id `task_<index>`, the row's image/cwd); until then a row naming an image fails with no model call and the parent warns up front.
- Intended differences recorded: D-16.271 … D-16.275, §2.7 (+ the shared §3.1 row with P21).
- Open seams: under the Docker backend an image row is still probed and **pulled before** the default registrar refuses it (wasted pull until P21-14); an orphaned worker can finish its current prompt after the parent is killed; converter goldens change only with a note entry; P22-10..15 (saver, compressor, SWE runner, evals kit, `research` extra with `transformers`/`datasets`) still to build — the `compress`, `swe`, `sample` verbs exit 1 with "later build".
- Test fix at landing (P14, also failing in the P15 landing run): `tests/integration/cron/test_m4_demo.py` advanced to the build job's next slot only, but the due jobs of one tick run side by side and each next slot counts from the end of its own run, so the blocked job's slot could land a few fake seconds later (failed 2 of 3 alone); the test now advances to the later of the two (asserting they are within a minute). 18/18 alone after the fix.
- Gate (this landing, verify3 tree on 2d0cbf9 + B1 + B2 + R1 + integration; shared by P21 and P22): ruff check/format, pyright (0 errors), lint-imports (1 kept) clean; `INDUS_STRESS_SECONDS=10 pytest -m "not perf"` run 2: 10866 passed, 11 skipped, 11 deselected (run 1: the one cron demo failure above, nothing else); `pytest -m perf` alone 11 passed; `tooling.checks --require-ref` ok; `CI=true pnpm test --maxWorkers=1` 855 passed (58 files); staged diff has no credential-shaped literal; no writes under `/root` (outside `.cache`, `.claude`, `.local/share/pnpm`), no stray processes (no fake apptainer instances) and no new `/tmp` entries.
## P15 waves 2–3 landing notes (lead) — P15-19, 20 (W1), P15-21..23 (W2), P15-24..26 (W3), P15-27..29 (W4), P15-30..32 (W5)
- Order applied on f83c19f (P00–P14, P15-01..18, P21-01..11/15, P22-01..09 landed), each `git apply --3way` from d0f29d0: W3 (clean), W1, W4, then W2, then W5. Conflicts, all unions: `foundation/config/schema.py` `GatewaySection` (W3 `connect_timeout_seconds` / `systemd_watchdog_seconds`, W1 `delivery_ledger`, W2 `stt_enabled`, W5 `api_server` / `webhook` sections) + its test, `cli/app.py` (`send` and W5's `webhook` row — one copy), help-order golden (`gateway`, `pairing`, `send`, `webhook`, `research`), non-TTY table (`send`, `gateway …`, `gateway api-key …`, `webhook …`; `gateway run/start/restart/install` under `long_running` — they start a gateway or register a real user service) (W4's `reconnect_attention_after` asserts kept in `test_gateway_rows_validate`), traps R06.17 / R06.18 (W3 and W4 tests), `gateway/daemon/__init__.py` (one docstring). `cli/app.py` (`send`, `gateway` rows), `core/admission.py` (W1 ledger key + R06.5 gate, W4 reconnect attribute + `post_gateway_admission` with the session id, W2 `front_doors`), contract tables, traps / acceptance rows merged clean (checked by hand).
- Lead rulings applied: (1) **one runtime-records module** `state/gateway_runtime.py` — W4's `service/gateway_runtime.py` folded in and deleted: `STATUS_FILE` = `gateway_state.json`, `runtime_view(home)` (W4's reader: running / stale / stopped / missing over the daemon's status file, liveness through `ProcessIdentity`), `alive(pid, start_time)`, one planned-stop record (`record_planned_stop(home, pid, reason, start_time=, by=)`, `PlanStop.matches`; the daemon labels its exit `planned_stop:<reason>` only when the record matches its pid **and** start time, then clears it), `stdio_log_path`; W1's ledger owner check uses `alive` too. (2)/(4)/(5) W4's `side_services.attach` runs inside **`ControlPart`** (required, `Stage.CONTROL`): `start` → `attach(DaemonBridge(daemon))`; `on_platform_down` → `platform_failed` (not for `stopping`; `lost=` when the platform had been up); `on_platform_up` → `watcher.forget`; new `daemon.on_ready(cb)` → `started(platforms)` (`gateway:startup`); `stop` → `services.stop()`. `DaemonBridge` maps `connect_platform` → `PlatformRuntime` → `ConnectOutcome` (`outcome_of`), the watcher's rows → `PlatformStatus` (a `None` row after a reconnect leaves the daemon's `connected` row alone), `identify` = `identity_payload` + `daemon.identity()`, and builds `drain` / `pause-for-update` / `reload-plugins` (async, on the loop) from the lifecycle and the hook folders. `RUN_TARGET` = `indus.gateway.daemon.command:run_command`. (6) `agent.cron_drain_timeout` (30 s) added as a row (W2 had a constant) and read by `extras.cron_drain_seconds`.
- W1 plugs in as W3's API says (`gateway/delivery/parts.py`): `LedgerPart` (RECOVERY; `sender_options["ledger"]`, `ledger.bind(replies.sender_for)`, `recovery.crash_reply_sink`, the sweep in `start`, `platform_reconnected` on a second `up`, `aclose` in `stop`; absent when `gateway.delivery_ledger` is false) and `RouterPart` (BACKGROUND; router attach/detach on up/down — a retrying platform's deliverer goes with its sender —, channel directory task, mirror). **Crash sink dedupe:** an owed reply the transcript holds is recorded in the ledger and sent with `RECOVERED_LABEL` unless `rows_for_session_key` already has a row created since the turn started (then the ledger sweep sent it): never twice.
- W5 plugs in as `http.part.HttpPart` (BACKGROUND; only when `gateway.api_server.enabled` or `gateway.webhook.enabled`): `start_http_surfaces(HttpHost(scope, services=daemon.surface_service, store=daemon.store, on_status=status.set_surface))`; `process.build_surface_service(daemon, spec)` (the chat builder split into `_session_service`): the spec's toolsets / answerable kinds / platform, MCP for the API server only, **no plugins** on either surface (one plugin host per profile), nothing for the untrusted webhook surface; tests pass `GatewayDaemon(surface_factory=)`. `RuntimeStatus.surfaces` (new; `StatusWriter.set_surface`): the API server's 30 s counters and each failed surface as `{problem}` — **a surface that cannot start never fails the daemon** (D-16.292). At stop the surfaces close, then their services. API-started cron runs (`/api/jobs/{id}/run`, `/api/cron/fire`) are now `ApiContext.jobs`, cancelled and awaited in `ApiServer.aclose` (test `test_api_server_runs.py::test_api_started_job_runs_are_cancelled_when_the_server_closes`). `indus gateway api-key` is a lazy row of the `gateway` group. `CompletionLog` kept (W1's ledger is for chat deliveries: platform + chat id + labelled redelivery; an HTTP background completion has neither) — D-16.290.
- W5 security review: one `BearerGate` wraps the whole API app (every route, `/health` included; verified by `test_http_part.py`), keys compared with `hmac.compare_digest` against every listed key (no early exit), the refusal log names method, path and client only; webhook signatures are checked on the raw body before parsing (GitHub HMAC, GitLab token, our generic HMAC-SHA256 with or without a 300 s timestamp window; all compared in constant time); `insecure` routes are refused unless the receiver binds loopback (route load); webhook turns check the session's tool list against `WEBHOOK_FLOOR` and refuse a wider one; the webhook surface's service gets no MCP or plugin tools.
- W2 plugs in as `extras.ExtrasPart` (BACKGROUND): `install_extras` in `prepare` through `ExtrasSeat` (W2's stand-in shape, now production), start hooks (cron host) at BACKGROUND start, stop hooks (cron drain, prompts and albums closed) at BACKGROUND stop. `PART_FACTORIES` = control, ledger, router, extras.
- **Security fix (W2 report):** a typed `/approve` / `/deny` that reaches the command handler without `accept()`'s front door (e.g. `pipeline.handle()`) now applies the same rule: never a bot (no answer), else the requester (`_who` of the session's origin, `pipeline.origin_source`) or a chat admin (`prompts.may_answer`, shared with the front door). Test: `test_prompts.py::test_a_typed_approval_that_skips_the_front_door_keeps_the_requester_rule` (fails without the fix).
- Other integration changes: `AdapterConfig` moved to `gateway/config.py` (re-exported by `daemon.process`); `indus send` builds adapters with it and loads plugin platforms (`PluginUpkeep(surface="cli")`, names registered with the target grammar) only when TARGET names no built-in; `ADAPTER_BUILDER` takes `(scope, name, settings, registry)`; `lifecycle.MAINTENANCE_REASONS` (`maintenance`, `operator`); the external drain marker only sets a drain when none is active and only clears its own; `indus gateway stop/restart` refuse when `_INDUS_GATEWAY` is set; spool fixture `die-on-send` marker.
- Stand-ins removed (no `# STAND-IN` first line left): `_stubs_w3.py` (process tests run `python -m indus gateway run`), `_stubs_w4.py` (CLI tests start/stop the **real** daemon over the spool plugin, directly and through the fake `systemctl`; the side-service unit tests use `tests/unit/gateway/daemon/ports.py`, a test double of the `DaemonPorts` protocol, and the real path is covered by `tests/integration/gateway/test_control_part.py`), both `_stubs_w2.py` (`ExtrasSeat`; `test_cron_delivery` goes through W1's router and sender), `_stubs_w5.py` (its two tests now run `HttpPart` in a real in-process daemon: `tests/integration/gateway/test_http_part.py`).
- New tests: `tests/integration/gateway/test_gateway_e2e.py` (real `indus gateway run` + spool plugin + HTTP fake model: DM answered and its ledger row `delivered`; `indus send spool` to the home channel; `indus gateway status` over the socket; SIGTERM → 0; die mid-send (exit 70) → restart delivers the stored reply **once** with the duplicate label, model called twice in all, no stray process or socket), `test_control_part.py` (identify/status, reconnect through `connect_platform`, drain / pause-for-update / reload-plugins, `gateway:startup` once).
- Dependencies (W5): `fastapi==0.142.2` (2026-09-30, MIT) and `uvicorn==0.54.0` (already locked via mcp; now a direct dependency). Re-locked with `uv lock` under the unchanged `exclude-newer = 2026-10-04`: package diff = **only additions, `fastapi` 0.142.2 and `annotated-doc` 0.0.5 (MIT)**, no version moved; `uv sync --frozen --all-extras` clean; licences checked by hand from the dist-info (`License-Expression: MIT` + LICENSE for both); THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES rows (fastapi, annotated-doc, uvicorn's purpose) and the layout §4 pin rows came with the patch.
- Intended differences recorded: D-16.276 … D-16.292, §2.7 (+ §3.1 row).
- Open seams: the daemon does not feed `StreamConsumer` / `ToolProgress` / `TypingLoop` / `Heartbeat` yet (replies go out whole); gateway chat sessions lack the learning loop (`with_learning` lives in `cli/runtime.py` — move to `service/`); `indus send` connects one-shot adapters, so P15-33..35 need a **send-only open** (Telegram long polling; the spool adapter's reader would also compete); G4's `identity_env()` names vs `child_env`; `reload-plugins` reloads hook folders only; adapters must call `extras.intake.register_fetcher` (P15-33..35); W1: a sender vanishing between claim and redelivery spends one attempt; W2: CLI/TUI approval cards onto `service/approval_copy.py`, cache age cleanup in housekeeping, Docker path translation for cached files, the cron ledger attempt shows ok while the job shows silent; W4: P17 `session-ticket` / `describe`, `agent:step` from `post_tool_call`; W5: reference routes with no phase row (`/health/detailed`, `/v1/capabilities`, `/api/model/options`, `/v1/skills`, `/v1/toolsets`, session create/patch/delete/messages/fork/chat), runs and stored responses lost on restart, the HTTP surfaces start after recovery (no API during the startup-restore window), `CompletionLog` is a file of its own; a route with `auth: auto` accepts the generic signature without a timestamp too (a captured request can be replayed until the secret changes: pin `hmac_ts` per route, or drop the unstamped form); ACC-K1-2..4, K34-1 rows missing.

## P21 B3 + P22 R2 landing notes (integrator) — P21-12 rest, P21-13, P21-14, P21-16 (B3); P22-10..15 (R2)
- Order applied on eb0bc9c (base of both patches adbbe87): B3, then R2, each `git apply --3way`. No conflicts (traps ledger, acceptance map, config schema and `test_schema.py` merged clean; R10.x and R02.x rows checked by hand). R2 adds config rows `research.save_trajectories` (false) and `research.sample_datasets` ([]).
- P21-14 wiring (stand-in removed: `tests/unit/research/_stubs_r2.py`; no `# STAND-IN` file left): `swe_runner.RegistryEnvironments.open` registers `TaskOverride(image=--image, backend=--env, cwd=<temp dir | --cwd | /tmp>)` in `backends.task_overrides.TASK_OVERRIDES` under `swe_<n>`; `_RegistryTask.run` calls the registry with the task id and **no call overrides**, so the registry plans the image per backend (before, `--image` reached the Docker factory as an `image` call override it never read); `close` releases the task environment, then clears the override and removes the temp folder in `finally`. The `OverrideRegistrar` / `PassThroughOverrides` seam is gone (the registry only consults the process-wide `TASK_OVERRIDES`, so an injectable registrar could only drift from it). The SWE runner has no session, so `bind` is not used there; the batch worker's `SessionAgentRunner` binds its session to `task_<index>` (B3) and `run_prompt` clears it in `finally`. R2's worker assumptions hold (R2 did not touch `worker.py`; image rows now run instead of failing — D-16.273 superseded). Tests: `test_swe_runner.py::test_registry_environments_use_per_task_overrides_and_release` (real registry: own `task` key, image as the Docker config overlay, cleared after release), `::test_registry_environments_clear_the_override_when_release_fails`.
- Security fixes at landing: (1) **a task cannot lift a pinned Docker image** — B3's first draft let an override image that names a digest replace `terminal.docker_image` under `docker_image_pinned: true`; `task_overrides.plan` now keeps the pinned image and ignores the task's image with one warning (the task still gets its own sandbox); test `test_task_overrides.py::test_a_pinned_docker_image_cannot_be_lifted_by_a_task` (D-16.322). (2) The `error` field of a failed SWE task's line was written raw; it now goes through the P05 redactor in force mode like the turns (`test_a_failed_tasks_error_line_is_masked`, D-16.328). Reviewed and found fine: vendor credentials reach SDKs only through the env parameters (B1/B2 path unchanged; contract test `test_values_ride_the_sdk_env_parameter_only`); setup stores answers only in the secret store (never `.env`), never echoes them, never stores the Vercel OIDC token; doctor prints credential names only; the saver redacts every turn value in force mode and holds a whole-file lock; the compressor's key is `summarization.api_key_secret` (a secret name, looked up in the store/env view), no literal; `trust_remote_code` false by default with a warning when on; the SWE runner refuses non-terminal tool calls and runs nothing for a call without a command; temp dirs removed in `finally`; the live lane needs `INDUS_LIVE_<VENDOR>=1` and prints only names.
- Dependencies: extra `research = ["datasets==5.0.1", "transformers==5.17.0"]`. Re-locked under the unchanged `exclude-newer = 2026-10-04`: **13 new packages** (annotated-doc 0.0.5 MIT, datasets 5.0.1 Apache-2.0, dill 0.4.1 BSD-3-Clause, multiprocess 0.70.19 BSD-3-Clause, pandas 3.0.6 BSD-3-Clause, pyarrow 25.0.1 Apache-2.0, regex 2026.9.29 Apache-2.0 AND CNRI-Python, safetensors 0.8.0 Apache-2.0, shellingham 1.5.4 ISC, transformers 5.17.0 Apache-2.0, typer 0.27.2 MIT, tzdata 2026.5 Apache-2.0, xxhash 4.0.1 BSD-2-Clause) and **one version moved: fsspec 2026.9.0 → 2026.6.0** (`datasets` 5.0.1 requires `fsspec[http]<=2026.6.0`; fsspec is otherwise only in the `voice` extra). Licences read from the installed dist-info (a throwaway venv in the scratchpad; the guard walks only `[project].dependencies`). None copyleft. **Owner checks** (same class as numpy's Zlib): `regex` declares `… AND CNRI-Python` (permissive, OSI, not on `licenses_allow.json`); the `pyarrow` wheels bundle Arrow's C++ dependencies, among them zlib (Zlib) and Boost (BSL-1.0), neither on the allow-list. THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES: rows for the 13, the fsspec row updated, `research` added to the rows the extra also reaches (huggingface-hub, hf-xet, filelock, tqdm, pyyaml, numpy, tokenizers, aiohttp family, requests, charset-normalizer, urllib3), and two rows that were missing before this landing (`packaging` 26.3, Apache-2.0 OR BSD-2-Clause; `colorama` 0.4.6, BSD-3-Clause, Windows only). CI installs no extras: the research tests use the fake tokenizer / injected loaders and pass without the extra.
- Test fix at landing (P15 file, pre-existing on main since c0e0201): `tests/unit/gateway/core/test_commands.py` imported PyYAML, which only the `voice` extra installs, so a no-extras venv (CI: `uv sync --locked`) failed at collection; it now uses `indus.foundation.yamlio` (`dump_roundtrip(to_roundtrip(...))`, `load_safe`). 22/22. **Shared with the P15 integration** — keep whichever side lands second consistent.
- Intended differences recorded: D-16.320 … D-16.329, §2.7 (+ §3.1 row; D-16.273 marked superseded by D-16.322).
- Open seams / follow-ups: the saver is wired for the CLI only (TUI bridge and gateway build their own services); `indus research swe` has no per-backend image flag (profile value unless `--image`); under a pinned Docker profile the batch worker still inspects/pulls a row's image before the override ignores it (wasted pull, no effect on the sandbox); delegation does not bind child sessions to task overrides yet; live smoke with real vendor credentials and ACC-D5-2 (real Apptainer overlay restart) still owner/lead items; the probe guards are process-wide (`reset_guards()` after a config or secret change is not wired to `indus setup`/`indus auth` yet).
- Gate (verify3, eb0bc9c + B3 + R2 + integration, no extras installed): ruff check/format, pyright (0 errors, 0 warnings after the `test_commands.py` fix), lint-imports (1 kept) clean; `INDUS_STRESS_SECONDS=10 pytest -m "not perf"` run 2: **11811 passed, 46 skipped, 11 deselected** (run 1 stopped at collection on the PyYAML import above, nothing else); `pytest -m perf` alone 11 passed; `tooling.checks --require-ref` ok; `CI=true pnpm test --maxWorkers=1` 855 passed (58 files); the two B3 load-only timing tests (ssh `test_home_detection_retried_no_sooner_than_30s`, core `test_three_hundred_turns_over_two_hours`) passed in the full run (the P15 integrator's full suite ran beside it); diff has no credential-shaped literal (incl. the Telegram token shape); no stray processes; no new `/tmp` entries during the full run — the 39 `indus-snap-*` / `indus_bg_proc_*` entries are all dated 2026-10-18 and no current test leaves one.

## P17 wave 1 landing notes (integrator) — P17-01..04 (K1: runtime ledger, admissions, durable turn lease, liveness watchdog), P17-05..07 (K2: generation fences, event log and replay, server-request re-delivery)
- Order applied on f15b020 (base of both patches 78be7b3): K1, then K2, each `git apply --3way` (direct-application fallback). One conflict, a union: traps R06.2 (K1's lease/ledger tests beside K2's fence test). `state/schema.py` (four `runtime_*` tables, new `turn_leases` columns), `state/deletion.py`, `foundation/errors.py` (`runtime.*`), `session_lock.py` docstring, `service/broker.py` (K2's `book=`, `srq-` ids), ACC-A63-1..3 / ACC-B25-1..2 merged clean (checked by hand). No dependency change, no re-lock.
- **Reconciliation** (stand-in `tests/unit/service/_stubs_k2.py` deleted; `grep -rln '^# STAND-IN' src tests` empty). K1's real interface differs from K2's assumption: `receipt` raises `LedgerRefusal("not_found")` (not None); `cancel` of an admission claimed meanwhile either interrupts that ticket itself and answers `started`, or (the ledger's own race) raises `admission_started`; an unknown one raises `unknown_execution`. K2's `stop` (not K1) adapted: it handles all three, reads the receipt again after a refusal, and counts `cancelled` only when the receipt's outcome is `cancelled` (a row steered or settled meanwhile is `already_settled`). `AdmissionReceipt` protocol gained `session_id` and `outcome`; K1's `Receipt` gained `session_id` (third field) so **`stop` answers `not_found` for an admission of another session** (before: `stop("s1", <s2's admission>)` cancelled s2's queued input, or interrupted s1's turn when the generation numbers matched — generations are per session). `test_fences.py` stop tests now run against `AdmissionService` with the fence wired (queued → cancelled; started → interrupted exactly its generation; finished → already settled, successor untouched; stale interrupt vs the real ticket; system interrupt recorded; other session → not_found; unknown after restart → unknown, nothing re-run; stop during a lease wait ends the wait, runner never called; queued-then-claimed through a subclass of the real service), plus `Receipts`, a small double of K1's real `receipt`/`cancel` (K1's `Receipt` and `LedgerRefusal`) for the races a store cannot be paused in (ledger `admission_started` refusal, released-after-read, early stop before `begin`, plain sync service).
- **Wiring done** (no P17-08 needed): `AdmissionService(fences=GenerationFences)`: `begin(session, generation)` at each claim, `adopt` at once with the ticket as `TurnControls` (`_FencedTurn`: another generation → no-op; soft → `interrupted`, hard and `stop` → `cancelled`, `by_system` → `interrupted_by_system` = `ExitReason.INTERRUPTED_BY_SYSTEM`), `release` when the turn has ended, before the settle. Adopting at claim (not when the runner binds) means the ticket is the stop latch, so a fenced stop also ends a lease wait; a steer before the runner binds its hook answers `rejected`. `GenerationFences.bind_admissions(service)` breaks the construction cycle (fence first, then the service with `fences=`, then bind).
- **One vocabulary**: K2's `request_redelivery.SettleOutcome` renamed `RequestSettlement` (the ledger already exports `SettleOutcome` for turn outcomes); `StaleGeneration.code` = the ledger refusal reason `stale_generation`, `StaleGeneration.error_code` = `runtime.stale_generation` (in `ERROR_CODES`; test); admission states `queued/started/terminal/unknown` are K1's and K2 uses them as is; generations are `int`, per session, from `runtime_generations` (shared by claims and leases, so the fence's "only grows" check holds across a `--local` lease); `request.settled` and `session.replay_gap` stay plain mappings until P17-25 declares them. Layers: `fences` imports `state.runtime_ledger`, `admissions` imports `safety.redaction` and `service.fences`; nothing in `service` imports `gateway`/`cli`; lint-imports kept.
- **Security review.** (1) Unknown outcomes: `begin_owner_epoch` flips every older-epoch `started` row to `unknown`, the FIFO pauses behind it, an exact retry answers the unknown receipt, a fenced stop answers `unknown`; tests `test_restart_parks_the_lost_turn_and_tells_once`, `test_fences.py::test_stop_of_an_unknown_admission_changes_nothing` (model call count 0 after restart). (2) Lease theft: a row is taken early only with proof — same host **and** same recorded pid namespace, then pid gone or start time differs; unknown start time, unreadable probe, another host/namespace, missing namespace, or this very process all keep the row until expiry; refresh/release are keyed by holder id (random per-lease nonce) and generation. New test `test_turn_lease.py::test_a_live_holder_cannot_be_declared_dead_without_proof`; the multiprocess file ran 5× green (3 passed each). (3) Fences: covered above, plus the cross-session hole fixed. (4) Event-log caps: per session 512 events / 4 MiB, 64 sessions, 64 MiB per process, oversized events never kept (gap), hypothesis tests under tight caps; not counted: held (≤33 ms) delta text and per-session counters (one int pair per session id, daemon life — K2 decision). (5) Re-offer filters by the viewer's renderable kinds and skips viewers that refused; a 4404 counts only from a viewer with the decline capability. (6) **Fix: details cleaned** — `runtime_ledger.clean_text` (every refusal field/detail and settle detail) now masks credential shapes (`foundation.redact.redact_text`) and cuts absolute POSIX/Windows paths to `…/<last part>` (URLs and relative paths kept); `AdmissionService` passes runner exceptions and runner-reported details through the P05 redactor in force mode with the store's scope first (the scope's own secret values). Before, a provider error carrying a key or a home path was stored verbatim in `runtime_admissions.detail`. Tests `test_runtime_ledger.py::test_refusal_detail_hides_credentials_and_paths`, `test_admissions.py::test_a_failure_detail_is_masked_and_paths_are_cut`.
- Intended differences recorded: D-16.360 … D-16.369, §2.7 (+ §3.1 row).
- **Wiring owed.** P17-08 (daemon host): the runner binds `ticket.bind(interrupt=, steer=)` to `SessionService` and honours `ticket.stop_reason` set before it bound; reload the session when `HeldLease.waited`; hard interrupt also stops subagents (in the host's interrupt hook); one `EventLog` per daemon (`run_flusher` task; `gateway.ready.replay_epoch = EventLog.epoch`); per session `RequestBook(sid, emit=elog.append, generation=fences.running)` → `ServerRequestBroker(book=)`, `fences.bind_requests(sid, book, broker.answer)`, `fences.forget` / `elog.forget` on close; `book.session_id` must follow compaction child ids. P17-13/14: viewers → `RequestViewer.of(connection id, kinds, send, can_decline=)`, client errors → `book.refuse`, `events.since` → `book.reoffer`; per-actor authorisation before any fence call; clarify locks. P17-18: `--local` takes the durable lease (then retire the P02 file lock). P17-22: messaging through `admit`, drain `gate=`, `resolve_unknown(terminal_write=)` closes the lost turn in the transcript, `on_blocked` wording, `/retry` as the gate's rewrite. P17-25: `runtime.*` and `stale_generation` onto the protocol table (`StaleGeneration.to_data()` is the error data), `request.settled` / `session.replay_gap` / `events.since` models, `stalled` as an `ExitReason` or not, retire `server_request.cancelled` once every surface reads `request.settled`.
- Follow-ups: backups still include the ledger tables (R03.21 says exclude); queued input is not rehomed on an automatic reset; a worker that meets a refusing gate exits (the drain-ender must kick it); redirect is not fenced; held delta text is outside the process cap; lease expiry trusts the acquirer's wall clock (a store shared across hosts on a network file system is out of scope); without `/proc/self/ns/pid` two containers with one hostname would share the `host:<name>` namespace id.
- Gate (verify6, f15b020 + K1 + K2 + integration, `uv sync --locked`, no extras): ruff check / format, pyright (0 errors, 0 warnings), lint-imports (1 kept) clean; `INDUS_STRESS_SECONDS=10 pytest -m "not perf"` **12442 passed, 46 skipped, 11 deselected** in one run (the wave-22 and another integrator's full suites ran beside it; no failure); `pytest -m perf` alone 11 passed; `tooling.checks --require-ref` ok; `CI=true pnpm test --maxWorkers=1` 855 passed (58 files); `test_turn_lease_multiprocess.py` 5× 3 passed; the later-added `test_a_live_holder_cannot_be_declared_dead_without_proof` passed with its file (55 passed with `test_fences.py`). Diff has no credential-shaped literal (fake keys in the new tests are built by concatenation); no stray process from this worktree; one `/tmp/indus-exec-*.pid` appeared during the run while two other full suites were running too (backends tests; not attributable, not from P17 code).

## P16-01 landing notes (lead) — inbound HTTP listener kit and identity helpers (slice E1)
- Landed alone ahead of the rest of wave 23 so the callback adapters (WhatsApp bridge/Cloud, SMS, LINE, WeCom callback,
  Buzz) can build on it. New files only (`gateway/adapters/_http.py`, `_identity.py`, `tests/fakes/platform/http_wire.py`,
  a sample "Hook" callback adapter in `tests/unit/gateway/adapters/http_sample.py` that runs the whole contract suite and
  is the template to copy) plus test references on trap R06.4.
- How a callback adapter uses the kit: `docs/notes/P16/http-kit.md` ("How a callback adapter uses the kit").
- No new packages (httpx, starlette, uvicorn already direct dependencies). Intended differences: D-16.350 … D-16.354.
- Follow-up (P15 core): `core/send.py`'s text-based classifier reads an httpx `ConnectTimeout` passed as `exc=` as an
  ambiguous never-retried timeout; adapters using the kit avoid it, but `SendResult.failure(exc=...)` callers with httpx
  exceptions still hit it.
- Fix landed with it (P08-13 cooldown, root cause of the intermittent `test_three_hundred_turns_over_two_hours` failure seen
  in the B3 run and this gate): `FallbackChain._arm` treated a provider reset that had *already passed* as "no reset" and
  armed a 60 s+ ladder step. Under load the fake-clock driver lets a few seconds pass between classification and arming, so
  the soak's 10 s Retry-After expired and the turn after the 429 storm stayed on the backup. The documented rule is "the
  provider's reset, else the ladder": a named reset now always wins (cooldown = max(reset − now, 0)); the notice says "retried
  at the next turn" under 1 s. Reproduced deterministically (30 slow awaits before `fall_back`: main fails, fix passes);
  new unit test `test_a_reset_that_already_passed_arms_no_ladder_step` fails without the fix.
- Second fix landed with it (P05-03 local backend, R02.12; root cause of an intermittent
  `test_grandchild_ignoring_sigterm_is_sigkilled` failure in the same gate): after the SIGKILL, `stop_tree` waited only for the
  leader, so a killed descendant could still be running (the kill takes effect when the target is next scheduled) when cleanup
  returned. It now waits, bounded by `REAP_WAIT_S`, until nothing of the tree is left (zombies excluded). Reproduced
  deterministically by delivering the SIGKILL 50 ms late (main fails); new test `test_cleanup_waits_for_a_late_sigkill`
  fails without the fix.

- Order applied on 46c6d7e (patch base 78be7b3), each `git apply --3way`: S, D, then T. Tests on both platforms (`slack/test_adapter.py::test_every_piece_card_and_status_of_a_channel_reply_goes_into_the_thread` fails without the flag; `discord/test_adapter.py::test_the_core_never_addresses_a_discord_thread_before_the_adapter_opens_it`). This was the occasional `test_signals.py::test_sigterm_mid_turn_…` failure under load. Regression test `test_boot_recovery.py::test_a_stop_after_the_reply_went_out_neither_interrupts_nor_resumes_the_turn` (fails without the fix).

- **Demo 3 (P15-36):** `tests/e2e/gateway/test_demo3.py` — one in-process daemon with every default part and the real three adapters over their wire fakes on a fake clock (kit `tests/integration/gateway/three_platforms.py`): same agent answers on all three; approval by a Telegram inline button (a stranger's press is answered and changes nothing; the card loses its buttons); SIGKILL inside the Telegram send after `attempting` (child `tests/e2e/gateway/demo3_child.py`) → restart → one reply labelled `DUPLICATE_PREFIX`, no model call, row `delivered`; cron "every 30m" → nothing at 29 min, one delivery to the Telegram home chat by 32 min, none more by 55 min; per platform: a 47 k-character reply in pieces within the cap with nothing lost, `/status` + `/stop` inline while busy, a stranger's DM never reaches the model. `tests/integration/gateway/test_three_platforms.py`: fetchers registered on up / removed on down for all three; revoked Telegram token → fatal, no more polls. `tests/e2e/gateway/live_demo3.py` (`network`, `INDUS_LIVE=1` + `INDUS_HOME`; never collected by default; credentials only via the profile's settings, outputs checked without echoing).
