- **P18-30 built (integrator; task note `docs/notes/P18/e2e-m6.md`, Read = the three reference test files' names only).** `tests/fakes/web_stack.py`: the whole stack per test in a temp dir — the scripted fake model, a `ProfileHome`, a real `indus gateway run`, a real `indus dashboard` — as a context manager and as a JSON-lines program (restart/stop/try the dashboard, run or attach the classic CLI in a pseudo-terminal, read the store or a home file, list/kill the dashboard's PTY children, quit → strays). `apps/web/e2e/fixtures.ts` drives it (`launch`, every stack closed and strays asserted at the end), reads/types xterm (`Term`), and an auto fixture logs every URL the browser asks for (page loads, fetches, WebSocket dials) with every credential it was handed (the token read from each served page, each minted WS ticket): at the end no URL may contain one or a `token=`/`ticket=` query. Specs (9, `pnpm web:e2e` = `typecheck:e2e && vite build && playwright test`; `apps/web/playwright.config.ts`, one worker, trace on failure): `auth.spec.ts` (token only in the header on every call, a bare call 401, `?token=` 401 beside a good header; a stale token after a dashboard restart reloads once, the guard cleared; a gated bind without a provider exits 1 and never listens; gated basic login: login page with its CSP, wrong password copy, sign in, no token in the page, a header token and a WS token credential refused, a ticket opens one socket once, sign out), `dashboard.spec.ts` (Sessions search with a marked snippet + delete, gone from the store; Config save lands in `config.yaml` and survives a reload; Logs shows the gateway log's tail as written), `chat.spec.ts` (below). `tests/integration/web/test_dashboard_daemon.py` runs the multi-surface check in the Python gate without a browser (`/api/pty` dialled as the SPA dials it, token handed over in `INDUS_DASHBOARD_SESSION_TOKEN` and offered only as a subprotocol; it is not in the PTY child's environment, nor in any URL or the dashboard's output). vitest excludes `e2e/**`; `tsconfig.e2e.json` (skipLibCheck: Playwright 1.56's `protocol.d.ts` uses `module` namespaces TypeScript 6 rejects); `.gitignore` `apps/web/test-results/`; root `web:e2e`; CI job `web-e2e` (uv sync, pnpm install, `playwright install --with-deps chromium`, TUI build, the suite, traces uploaded on failure) added to the gate's `needs` (R10.18) — not run in CI from here.
- **Fixes the browser run found (root-caused, each pinned).** (1) **Login impossible in a real browser** (P18-28): the login page sent `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`; under it Chromium sends `Origin: null` on the form's POST and the gate's CSRF check (`same_origin`) refused every sign-in with 403 `forbidden_origin` (TestClient never sends an Origin, so the unit tests passed). Now `same-origin` (header + meta): the POST names our origin, the referrer still never leaves it; `test_login_page.py::test_the_form_post_keeps_its_origin` (header, meta, a same-origin POST signs in, `Origin: null` still 403) and `auth.spec.ts` (D-16.656). (2) **Garbage in every prompt typed in the browser** (P12 TUI): the renderer probes the background with OSC 11 and gives up after 150 ms; xterm.js answers over the WebSocket later, and the answer (`ESC ] 11;rgb:eded/ebeb/e8e8 ESC \`) went through Ink as typing — `11;rgb:…` in the input line and in the message the model got. `apps/tui/src/terminal/inputFilter.ts` now drops complete OSC replies, also split across reads (an open `ESC ]` tail is held like the other partial sequences); `keys.test.ts::drops a late OSC colour reply, whole or split across reads, and keeps the keys around it` (D-16.657). (3) **Stale text on screen for 30 s after every resume or reload** (P18-26): the resume sanitiser dropped every `ESC [ n K`/`ESC [ n X` in its window, and Ink repaints each line with `ESC [ 2 K`, so a closed approval card stayed drawn and lines overlapped. Erase codes are now dropped only inside a flood (a run of breaks and erase codes holding ≥ 50 breaks, folded into one blank line); outside one they pass; the held trailing run includes erase codes between its breaks. My first rewrite had ambiguous `\r?\n|\r` alternatives under `+…$` and backtracked exponentially on long runs (a vitest run hung) — alternatives are now `\r\n|\n|\r(?!\n)`. `resumeSanitizer.test.ts` two new + three updated, `ptySocket.test.ts` the two resume cases updated; R09.8's flood tests unchanged and green (D-16.653).
- **Fixes from the full runs (root-caused, test-only, product unchanged).** (1) Full run 2 hung for good at `tests/unit/tools/builtin/code/test_kernel.py::test_timeout_kills_the_kernel_and_the_next_call_starts_fresh` (0 CPU, 14 min). Stacks taken from the live process (gdb → `faulthandler.dump_traceback`, `$S/p18c-hang-stacks.txt`): the main thread sat in the autouse teardown `_rig.shutdown_pools` → `KernelPool.shutdown` → `LocalKernel.close` → `stop_tree`, which waits on the kernel's own clock — the test's `FakeClock` — while the child is still running; in a teardown nothing advances it, so whenever the runner had not yet exited when its pipe closed (a loaded machine) the suite stopped forever (the kernel already a zombie, never polled again). Reproduced deterministically by holding the child stopped (`SIGSTOP`) before the teardown: the old helper hangs (30 s bound hit), the new one closes it. `_rig.shutdown_pools` now closes each pool while moving the fake clocks its kernels were made with (`_close_pool`); new `test_kernel.py::test_teardown_closes_a_fake_clock_kernel_whose_child_has_not_exited` 3/3; the code-tool suite 91 passed. (2) Full run 1 failed once at `tests/unit/gateway/adapters/wecom/test_adapter.py::test_media_goes_up_in_chunks_with_wecom_limits` ("never finished on the fake clock"; passed alone 3/3): the test's `drive` spins 4000 fake 0.5 s steps with no real wait, while the adapter reads the media on a worker thread (`run_blocking(file.read_bytes)`, `adapter.py:856/874`) — under load the 4000 iterations pass before the thread hands its bytes back. `drive` now also gives the task 1 ms of real time per step (`asyncio.wait({task}, timeout=0.001)`); the WeCom suite 37 passed. Same family as the lead's `test_batch` fix.

- **Security review.** (1) No credential in any URL, checked from the browser's side on every spec (request + WebSocket log against the served token and every minted ticket) and from the server's in the Python test; WS credentials only in `Sec-WebSocket-Protocol`; the handed-over session token is not in the PTY child's environment. (2) Gated: the login page has no script (CSP `default-src 'none'` + style hash), the referrer change keeps the referrer same-origin only and the CSRF check unchanged (`Origin: null` still refused); a header token and a WS token credential are refused; tickets single use. ## P20 wave 34 landing notes (integrator) — P20-08..14 backend ladder, installer runner, local attach + tickets, remote, boot orchestrator, supervision, REST proxy (w34-m); P20-15..18 renderer entry/shell, boot overlay + first-run + failure, connection store, transcript (w34-r); P20-19..24 composer, journals, busy actions + queue, cards, model picker + status bar, slash (w34-c)

- **Order applied** on main 3ba165d (worktree `int-p20a`; patch base 9a1917d), each `git apply --3way` (pnpm-lock excluded from C and M, see pnpm): w34-r, w34-c, w34-m. R's lock hunk conflicted with main's lock and was dropped for the re-lock. C against R: conflicts in C's four STAND-IN files and the three shared desktop files; M: one conflict in `electron/main.ts`. Resolved by reading both sides: `package.json` (R's deps + C's `dump:slash` script; react/testing-library/jsdom pins identical), `tsconfig.json` (`resolveJsonModule` + `types: [node, vite/client]`), `vitest.config.ts` (R's `src/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}`), `main.ts` (C's `installJournalIpc` then M's `installBackend`, both before `registry.seal()`). `grep -rn STAND-IN apps packages src`: none; no conflict markers.
- **Stand-ins dropped, C reconciled with R** (no first-answer-wins weakened): C's `gateway/connectionStore.ts`, `gateway/serverRequests.ts`, `i18n/index.ts`, `i18n/en.ts` removed for R's modules. R's `en.ts` spreads `...CHAT_STRINGS` (its duplicate `status.label` dropped; same text). Thin adapter in R's modules: `connectionStore.ts` adds `ChatConnection` (the part of `GatewayConnection` the chat surfaces use; the class `implements` it, tests fake it), `REQUEST_TIMEOUTS` (same numbers as `timing.ts`), `RequestOptions`, `SessionEvent`, `ConnectionMode`; session listeners are typed as server events (only events with a `session_id` reach them). `serverRequests.ts` adds `OpenServerRequest` = `PendingServerRequest` + `answer` bound by `bindAnswers` to `ServerRequestStore.answer(id, …)` — the store still refuses a second answer. `react.tsx`: `useGatewayConnection()` (explicit `GatewayConnectionProvider` value, else the primary connection, else null — R's alias threw without a store), `useServerRequestStore()`, `useOpenServerRequests(sessionId | null)` (bound), `ServerRequestStoreProvider`. C's files import these (`GatewayConnection` type → `ChatConnection`; `i18n/index.ts` → `index.tsx`); C's card test drops `generation`, adds `receivedAt`. The status bar hides the version while `codeVersion` is unknown; the composer treats an unknown mode as remote (bytes).
- **Slots mounted** (`src/app/chatSlots.tsx`, from `main.tsx`): ChatView `composer` (C's Composer with navigation on create / gone / `/new`, `/reasoning show|hide` → `TranscriptStore.setShowReasoning`, slash output lines above the composer, the transcript's local rows), `cards` (`<CardStack sessionId>` in the transcript footer), Shell `statusBar` (C's StatusBar for the route's session, beside R's connection badge — Shell renders the slot in a `div` host since StatusBar brings its own `<footer>`), and a new `retry` slot (ChatView `onRetry`; R's default actions kept for the rest): Retry sends the failed turn's message again as a new input. The person's own message: no daemon event carries it, so `src/chat/localEcho.ts` adds R's optimistic row (`addLocalUser`) before `prompt.submit` and binds it to the started turn (dropped when queued, steered or refused).
- **M's bridge contract applied.** R's cast on `connection.ticket` dropped (`ConnectionBridge = Pick<IndusDesktopConnection, "get" | "ticket" | "onChanged">`, default bridge through `desktopBridge()`); the boot overlay listens on `boot.onNotice` (`indus:backend:notice`) and `BackendNotice` is the generated type. Bridge types regenerated (`gen-bridge-types --check` clean); `check-ipc` clean; C's journal shapes and M's notice row and ticket `protocols` both in `channels.ts` / `schemas.ts` (they touched different rows; merged clean). Unions checked: `apps/desktop/{package.json, tsconfig.json, vitest.config.ts}`, `electron/main.ts`, `src/global.d.ts`.
- **"surface: gui".** `session.create` has no `surface` field — the daemon takes the surface from `client.capabilities` (`conn.subs.configure`). The connection store now sends `client.capabilities {features: ["server_requests", "request.decline"], surface: "gui"}` on every new socket before its replay. Without it the daemon also offered the window no approval/clarify/sudo/secret cards (the TUI announces `server_requests` the same way) (D-16.674).
- **pnpm.** `pnpm install` re-locked (+1230 lines, 158 new packages, none removed), then `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` accepts it ("Lockfile passes supply-chain policies"; node-pty's optional build skipped for lack of node-gyp as before). Electron: `ELECTRON_SKIP_BINARY_DOWNLOAD=1`, the cached `electron-v44.5.1-linux-x64.zip` unpacked into `node_modules/electron/dist` + `path.txt`. Licence / age check of every new package (`npm view`, cached in `$S/int-p20a-npmview/`, table in `$S/int-p20a-licences.txt`): shiki 4.5.0 (+ `@shikijs/*` 4.5.0) MIT 2026-10-01; katex 0.19.0 MIT 2026-10-01; lucide-react 1.52.0 ISC 2026-10-04; mermaid 11.17.2 MIT 2026-08-25 and its tree: 113 MIT, 35 ISC, 6 BSD-3-Clause (d3-array 2, d3-ease, d3-path 1, d3-sankey, d3-shape 1), 1 Apache-2.0 (`@chevrotain/types`), 1 Unlicense (`robust-predicates`), `dompurify` 3.4.16 `(MPL-2.0 OR Apache-2.0)` (both allowed; taken as Apache-2.0), `khroma` 2.1.0 with no npm licence field — its `license` file is MIT. Newest: `package-manager-detector` 1.9.0, 2026-10-05 (≥ 14 days before 2026-10-20). No disallowed licence. Rows added to 00-layout §4 (shiki, katex, mermaid with its tree, lucide-react). `tooling.checks licenses` ok.
- **Wired smoke → test.** `testing/electron/wiredApp.electron.test.ts` (+ `testing/electron/cdp.ts`, a small DevTools-protocol driver): builds the renderer and main into a temp app outside the checkout (so the resolver takes `INDUS_DESKTOP_INDUS`, not this checkout's runtime), launches Electron 44.5.1 under xvfb with `--remote-debugging-port`, and drives it with real input events. Fake backend: boot → connected → `client.capabilities {surface: "gui"}` on the wire → "Hello fake" typed + Enter → route `#/s-fake-1`, the person's row shown → three scripted events stream "Hello from the fake daemon." → a scripted approval card → "Allow once" clicked → card closed, exactly one `server_request.answer {choice: once}` on the wire. Real runtime: `tests/fakes/web_stack.py` (real `indus gateway run`, scripted model) → the desktop resolves `.venv/bin/indus`, attaches, the prompt streams "Streaming from the daemon.", the `terminal` tool's approval card is answered, the next model turn prints "Cleaned up."; nothing left running (`stray == []`). Both pass (12.7 s together); they skip without an Electron binary / display, the real case without `.venv`. Scratch driver + screenshots: `$S/int-p20a-smoke/`.
- **Fixes the wired run found (root-caused, each pinned).** (1) **Nothing started the boot**: R's store dialled only after the overlay reported ready, and `boot.get` only reads progress (M's code; its note says it starts an attempt) → the window sat on "Looking for Indus on this computer" forever. `main.tsx` now starts the primary connection at launch (`connection.get` starts or joins the boot); a boot failure still leaves the store `failed` until the overlay's ready (D-16.675). (2) **CSP blocked the daemon**: Electron 44 applies main's header to the `file://` page (R's note assumed not), and the header's `connect-src 'self'` was fixed at load, before the daemon's origin was known → "Connecting to ws://127.0.0.1:… violates connect-src 'self'". The header (`CspState`) now carries the meta's runtime sources (`RUNTIME_CONNECT_SOURCES`, moved to `electron/security/csp.ts`) beside the exact origins; test `csp.test.ts::CspState's header lets a page loaded before the boot reach the backend it finds` (D-16.670). (3) **Daemon refused the Origin (4403)**: inside Electron `onBeforeSendHeaders` does not show `Sec-WebSocket-Protocol` for a WebSocket upgrade (seen: User-Agent, Upgrade, Connection, Origin, Sec-WebSocket-Version, Accept-*), so M's reservation lookup by ticket marker never matched and `Origin: file://` reached the daemon. `DialReservations.rewrite` now matches a local dial by window + exact URL when the header is absent (oldest live reservation, single use; the daemon still redeems the ticket); test `tickets.test.ts::inside Electron (no subprotocol header) a local dial is matched by window and exact URL` (D-16.684). (4) **A needless redial per launch**: main announces the backend (`connection:changed`) when its boot ends, before the window's own `connection.get` answers; the store redialled and kept `lastError: "the backend changed"` while open. Not a change now; test `connectionStore.test.ts::a backend announced while the first dial waits for its descriptor is not a change` (fails without the fix). (5) **The first message of a new chat showed "Not confirmed … Retry / Dismiss" while it ran** (seen on the real run's screenshot): the fresh draft sent through a throw-away `ChatActions`; after the route change the composer's own instance ran `recoverPrepared()`, listed the journal while `prompt.submit` was in flight and kept the entry as uncertain for good. Now one `ChatActions` per (connection, session) (`sharedActions`, used by the composer's fresh-draft send, the composer showing the session and Retry), and recovery skips inputs that object is sending or has settled; tests `busyActions.test.ts::listing the journal mid-submit never offers the input being sent` (fails without the fix), `::one actions object per session and connection`, and the wired test asserts no "Not confirmed" while the card is up.
- **Remote token swap (M's decision) — does not work inside Electron.** Checked with a probe app on Electron 44.5.1 (`$S/int-p20a-smoke/swap/`): `onBeforeSendHeaders` can delete `Origin`, but a rewritten `Sec-WebSocket-Protocol` is ignored — the server received `indus-dashboard-v1, indus-dashboard-ticket.RESERVATION`, not the swapped token. A real serve in token mode accepts only the token itself in the subprotocol (gated mode only a ticket), so remote chat in the desktop is **owed**. Owner question D-16.691 (§3.2) with the recommendation (the serve mints a single-use ticket for a token-mode client; main mints one per dial; the renderer offers it like the local ticket) or a main-side relay. The remote HTTP leg (REST proxy) is unaffected.
- **Lead decisions applied.** Stand-ins dropped, C on R's API with a thin adapter in R's modules (above); slots mounted; `surface: "gui"` sent (on `client.capabilities`, not `session.create`); M's cast dropped, notice row wired, bridge types regenerated; unions re-locked; wired smoke turned into a test; remote swap verified (fails, recorded). Builders' decisions numbered D-16.670 – D-16.689 (§2.7) + §3.1 range row; owner questions D-16.690 (installer release base — M's guess `indusagi/indus-agent`, repo today `varunisrani/Indus-agent`) and D-16.691 (remote dial) in §3.2; D-16.692 – D-16.699 unused.
- **Security review.** Credentials: the only thing crossing the bridge is still the single-use local ticket (`connection.ticket`); the serve token and the remote token stay in main (the remote swap simply does not happen in Electron, so the token never reaches the wire from the renderer either); no credential in any URL (WS URLs carry none; the fake daemon refuses a query). The new local-dial match strips `Origin` only for a dial from the reserving window to the exact reserved URL within 25 s, once; the daemon still needs the ticket (no auth change). CSP: only `connect-src` widened in main's header, to exactly what the meta already allowed (loopback any port + `wss:`/`https:`); script/frame/object/base rules unchanged (the dev-server header loses its "exact origins only" narrowing — dev only). `client.capabilities` carries no credential. Cards: first answer wins in the one store (`react.test.tsx::open requests come bound to the store: the first answer wins, a second is refused`; wired test: one answer on the wire). No `hermes`/`nous` names, no credential-shaped literals (fake tokens in the new tests are built at runtime or are plain words), nothing written under `/root` or the real `~` (HOME, INDUS_HOME, caches, pnpm store, Electron cache, TMPDIR all in the scratchpad).
- Intended differences recorded: D-16.670 – D-16.689 (§2.7) + §3.1 range row D-16.670 – D-16.699; §3.2 D-16.690, D-16.691. - **P20-40** (`apps/desktop/e2e`, `pnpm --filter @indus/desktop e2e`; note `docs/notes/P20/e2e-demo4.md`; D-16.726): our DevTools driver under vitest, Electron 44.5.1 (release zip via the proxy, SHA-256 checked against SHASUMS256.txt, unpacked into `node_modules/electron/dist` + `path.txt`) under `xvfb-run`; OS browser = an `xdg-open` recorder on PATH. 6 files / 37 tests: **boot** (healthy boot as a gui viewer; latched failure overlay; first run: chooser → fake-installer stages → managed runtime → connected), **chat** on a real daemon + scripted model (no overlay for a keyless custom endpoint; onboarding re-run from Settings through the real `/api/model/test-endpoint`; deep link → Settings → default folder; settings search "proxy" over the real schema; streamed reply + write_file tool row + approval answered once; git review / files / preview panes; transcript-once after reload; second chat and back via the sidebar; profile rail switch to a new profile with its own daemon and back), **lifecycle** (no daemon first: the app starts one; `kill -9` → one notice, every frame duplicate-free, next prompt answered; quit; three relaunches; nothing left), **security** (4 hostile guests in the real preview; trusted-click positive control; fs confinement through symlinks; terminal env floor; deep-link refusal/confirmations; media roots; link previews refuse private hosts; diagnostics redacted; CSP refuses an injected inline script), **surfaces** (HUD pop-out; shortcut registration + refusal; tray without and with a StatusNotifier host — private session bus + `testing/fakeTrayWatcher.py`: hide on close, back when the host leaves; update check against a loopback feed; first-run onboarding by API key, backend only), **packaged** (`--dir` build, fuses verified, archive checked, packaged app interactive ≤ 60 s with CSP). CI: new `desktop-e2e` job (ubuntu, xvfb/dbus/python3-gi, 1 retry) in the gate's `needs`; packaged smoke added to `desktop-package` (Linux). Not runnable here / not built: signed installs (P23), macOS and Windows lanes, a real global-shortcut keypress and the quick-entry window (no X-level input injection; registration asserted), clarify/deny, steer/queue/WS drop mid-stream under the sampler, lineage and remote topology specs (remote chat owed, D-16.691).
- **Product fixes the E2E lane found (root-caused, each pinned):** (1) a reloaded or reopened conversation showed nothing on a real daemon — its WebSocket does not serve `session.history` (P17 contract test) — now read over REST through main's proxy (`src/chat/restHistory.ts`, D-16.718); (2) a session opened before the connection was ready never loaded its history — loads at ready (`Transcript.test.tsx::a session opened while the connection dials loads its history once it is ready`); (3) after `kill -9` the reply showed twice for ~100 ms (`applyHistory`, D-16.719, unit test fails without the fix); (4) the "restarted" notice was never visible (overlay gone by then) → toast (D-16.720); (5) a keyless custom endpoint re-showed onboarding every launch (D-16.722); (6) onboarding opened from Settings rendered under the settings dialog → Settings closes first; (7) the tray toggle as first wired was disabled whenever the tray was off → stays switchable, says why when refused; (8) the sessions list and the profile rail loaded once before the boot finished and stayed empty → reload at ready (D-16.724); (9) the review/files panes refused the session folder ("outside this window's folders") — nothing declared the workspace roots → the shell does (D-16.717); (10) after `kill -9` the supervisor's respawn and the reconnect's ticket mint each ran `gateway ensure` and two daemons could start (the loser exited by itself; once the reply after the restart never came) → overlapping ensures share one run (D-16.727, `localAttach.test.ts::overlapping ensures …`, fails without the fix; lifecycle passes alone 3/3 after it); (11) the terminal said "native module missing" in a copied app (harness: the e2e app copy links `node_modules`). Harness: DevTools calls bounded; attach retries a guest caught mid-navigation (the one lane flake: security's trusted-click attach, passes alone 3/3 after the fix).
- **Security review.** IPC: no raw handler (`check-ipc`), every channel through the registry's zod schemas; no new channel at integration. fs/media: confined before and after symlinks (E2E), media roots extended only with roots the guard accepted (never removed until restart — images only; follow-up). Preview guests: no bridge, no popups, untrusted clicks/`file:` refused, trusted https opens once through the audited opener (E2E). Deep links: no command MCP servers, confirmation before any install, Cancel installs nothing (E2E + REST log). Terminal env floor (E2E). Diagnostics: redacted (E2E: no provider key, no home path); the connection's serve/remote token now passed as a known secret. Credentials over the bridge: still only the single-use local ticket; REST history goes through main's proxy (the renderer never sees the serve token). Link previews: private hosts refused (E2E: the loopback server got no request). Credential-literal scan of the staged diff: clean (fake keys built at runtime); nothing written under `/root` or the real `~` (HOME/XDG/uv/npm/Electron caches in `$S/int35`, pnpm store `pnpm-store-p20`, TMPDIR unset); no stray processes (mine stopped by pid; the specs now stop processes under their own temp homes).

Aligned on K1: sandbox suffix `-slim`, so aliases are `stable-slim`, `latest-slim`, `canary-slim` (and `-browser`), and the schema default is `:stable-slim`.
- **Install settings key (D-16.748).** Both installers now write U1's `updates.installs.<id16>.channel` (explicit channel) and `updates.installs.<id16>.extras` (the list they synced, on every install, as `[a, b]` through `indus config set --force`); their readers (`ins_install_setting`, `Get-IndusInstallSetting`) read `updates.` and parse lists, and a later install leaves out the browser group when the saved list lacks it. `.skip` removed. Tests on both sides: the sh config stage's saved value is read back by the installer and by `indus.cli.update.channels.saved_extras`.

The digest outputs are not consumed: `publish.py` reads the digests back from GHCR by the attempt-ref tags. Workflow tests updated (AFTER_GATE set, interface equality with docker.yml's inputs).

Note for the P20 integration: P20-38's stamp falls back to the root `pyproject.toml` version, which is now dynamic — it must read `src/indus/__init__.py` (`__version__`) or always get `INDUS_DESKTOP_VERSION` (ci.yml's `desktop-package` job), and the stamp's commit must be the admitted commit, not `GITHUB_SHA`, in release runs.

I2's uninstall takes the install kind from `indus.cli.update.detect_kind` (drop-in; also the desktop runtime and dev-container clones). **Update marker (D-16.761)** — `src/indus/cli/update/marker.py`: `indus update` claims `<root>/.indus-update-in-progress` `{format: 1, owner: "indus-update", pid, process_start, created_at}` under the update lock before the move (temp file + `os.link`, so it appears complete; `O_EXCL` fallback), `process_start` in unix seconds (`/proc` btime + ticks on Linux, `ps lstart` on macOS, null on Windows where no pid is ever probed with `os.kill`); a live marker of another pid refuses the run, a stale one is replaced under the lock; released when apply returns and, after the exec hand-off (same pid), at the end of `--finish`. Tests: `tests/unit/cli/update/test_marker.py` (body, refusal, replacement, owner-only release, the desktop's live/stale table, real start times) and three command-level tests in `tests/integration/update/test_update_apply.py`. `<root>` is the root home (where the update lock and receipts live); D4 reads `<home>`, the same folder for the default profile.

The release `nix` lane also runs `nix run`. The checks job also runs `python3 scripts/docker/pins.py check` and `scripts/nix/pins.py --check`. Guards (D-16.767): banned scans `docker/` and `nix/`; similarity scans `docker/`, `nix/`, `.github/` — both ok (`.github` stays out of the banned scan: the workflows name the pinned reference URL).
- **Network steps.** `uv lock` (done). `pins.py update --uv 0.11.32 --git 2.51.0.windows.1` (done: 10 digests; uv digests equal upstream `.sha256`; both PortableGit assets exist, 59 MB each; the release page/API were blocked here, so Git for Windows digests are from the downloads only; newer GfW exists, 2.56.0.windows.2). `scripts/docker/pins.py refresh`: done in two steps: the bundle digests (s6-overlay ×4, Node ×2, hadolint ×2, actionlint ×2, each verified against upstream checksum files), then — after Docker Hub's anonymous limit of the shared egress (429, `ratelimit-remaining: 0;w=3600`) had reset — the `debian:trixie-slim` index digest (`sha256:62bd423a…`), rendered into both Dockerfiles; `pins.py check --strict` passes. Action SHAs verified with `git ls-remote` (gh/API calls are blocked here): all correct except U1's `actions/checkout` v7.0.1 (`f548e57…` is not that tag) → corrected to `3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1` in release.yml and canary.yml.
- **Security review.** Installers: every download needs a pinned SHA-256 (sh refuses an empty digest before downloading; ps1 refuses a non-64-hex pin), the mirror only after curl network codes 5/6/7/18/22/28/52/55/56 (ps1: network-class exceptions), digest checked whatever the origin; **fixed**: install.sh downloads now `--proto '=https' --proto-redir '=https'` (ps1 already refused non-https). No `eval` / `iex` of remote data inside the scripts (the `irm | iex` lines are the documented entry, the cmd shim runs the released script); release tags read from the API/redirect are matched by regex before use; values from config are read by awk/PS readers, never executed. Uninstall removes only planned paths (`_remove_tree` unlinks symlinks without following, refuses a code dir that contains the home or is `~`), PATH blocks between exact markers, gateways stopped by recorded pid (`taskkill /PID` only for the extract child it started); no pkill. Update keeps `.venv-prev`, refuses managed kinds, holds a lock + marker. Release tests use `tests/fakes/release/*` only (no network; `release.py` dry-run only). No secret values: workflows name secrets only; credential-literal scan of the staged diff clean.

- **F-1 fixed (D-16.960, must).** `release.yml`'s `upload` job attached only `desktop-*` artefacts, so `install.sh`, `install.ps1` and `install.cmd` were never release assets while the install guide and `install.cmd` fetch them from `releases/latest/download/…` (404). - **Found by the gate (D-16.1179).** `tests/unit/gateway/rooms/test_watch.py::test_watchers_get_every_new_room_event_in_order_once` failed once in the full run (connection two got `message.user, message.member`, missing one message) and **reproduced alone 1 in 20** — in wave 47's `gateway/rooms/watch.py`, which no wave-50 slice touches. Cause: a `groups.watch` from an earlier cursor landing while a poll's read ran off the loop took that page's newer events, so its cursor jumped past the older ones (a real gap, not a test artefact). Fixed: `RoomWatch._poll_room` gives a page read from `since` only to watchers whose cursor is at least `since`; the newcomer is served from its own cursor by the next poll (its watch's nudge makes that immediate). New deterministic test `test_a_watcher_added_while_a_read_runs_misses_no_event` fails on the wave-47 code and passes now; `test_watch.py` 50/50 after the fix; `tests/unit/gateway/rooms` + `tests/unit/protocol/test_models.py` 504 passed; note `docs/notes/P24/room-driver.md` gained a section.
